=== add ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md
new 6e49720dd5761319b23c033b
+# One process, many window servers
+
+Decided by the developer, 2026-11-18. They often have six windows open. Each window was its own node process with
+its own per-minute token budget, blind to the others, and the bookkeeper ran so rarely the checklist fell behind.
+
+> "Let's do one process with multiple servers. We can have one prioritized queue for the LLM calls and for global
+> resources. Have just one instance. Let's also change anything possible to be asynchronous if it's synchronous right
+> now. And let's take advantage of the fact that we have one single process now however we can."
+
+Four read-only surveys (2026-11-18) fed this: what state collides, what blocks the event loop, every model call and
+what triggers the side agents, and how a window is launched, locked and killed.
+
+## 1. Shape
+
+- **The host** (`src/host.mjs`, new): the one node process. It owns everything process-wide: the servers, signals,
+  the crash handler, exit, the LLM scheduler, the worker pool, the plugin manager and filter pipeline, the token
+  store, logs' masking, the settings bus, the sub-agent cap.
+- **A window server**: one `startServer(...)` call, as today, on its own port (each window keeps its own origin - the
+  browser's ~6 connections per origin and the tab leader election of build 144 depend on it), with its own project.
+  It is handed the host's services (`host` option) instead of making its own.
+- **One instance.** The first launch starts the host; every further window - a launch, "Open a new window", a
+  shortcut - asks the host for another server. Started as `node code-boss.mjs host`.
+
+### Host control API
+
+Bound to 127.0.0.1 on `CB_HOST_PORT` (default 18887 outside the container; the manager picks inside). Every request
+carries `X-CB-Host-Key: <CB_HOST_KEY>` (a random key the starter writes into the host's environment and keeps); a
+request without it is refused. Registry file `~/.code_boss/host.json` `{ pid, port, build, startedAt }` (no key in it;
+the starter that made the key keeps it - the manager in its own state, the launcher in `~/.code_boss/secure/`).
+
+| Call | Does |
+|---|---|
+| `GET /host/health` | `{ pid, build, uptime, servers: [{ id, port, project, windows, status, busy }] }` |
+| `POST /host/servers` `{ port, instanceId?, browserPort?, windowProfile?, windowHost?, restartKey?, managerUrl?, runOut? }` | starts a window server; answers `{ id, port }` once it listens |
+| `DELETE /host/servers/<id>?why=...` | flushes and closes that server only |
+| `POST /host/quit?why=...` | flushes every server, exits 0 |
+
+What was per-process environment for a window (`PORT`, `CB_INSTANCE_ID`, `CB_BROWSER_PORT`, `CB_WINDOW_PROFILE`,
+`CB_WINDOW_HOST`, `CB_RESTART_KEY`, `CB_MANAGER_URL`, `CB_RUN_OUT`, `CB_RESTARTED_WHY`) becomes the body of
+`POST /host/servers` and a `startServer` option. Nothing writes `process.env` per window any more.
+
+### Who starts it
+
+- **Container (production):** the Java manager (`sandbox-launcher/.../App.java`) starts the host once (env
+  `CB_HOST_PORT`, `CB_HOST_KEY`, `CB_IN_CONTAINER=1`), publishes its control port, and turns "start instance n" into
+  `POST /host/servers` on instance n's inside port (still published one by one), "stop instance n" into
+  `DELETE /host/servers/<id>`. Its health watch asks `/host/health`; a host that does not answer for 3 min is
+  restarted (every window), never one window. Chrome per window stays as is (`chrome-container-<port>`).
+- **Outside the container (dev, tests):** `launch-cmd.mjs` finds the host by `host.json` + `/host/health`, starts it
+  if there is none, then `POST /host/servers` on a free port and opens Chrome on that port's profile; when that
+  Chrome exits it sends `DELETE /host/servers/<id>`.
+- **`serve` stays** (one server in a host of its own) so tests and `CB_ALLOW_OUTSIDE_CONTAINER` runs work unchanged.
+
+## 2. Decisions taken (defaults; the developer can overrule)
+
+1. **A project already open in another window** is refused as today ("open in another window", with Force open).
+   The lock is keyed by server (pid + port), and the host also keeps project -> server in memory.
+2. **One window closing closes its server only.** `/api/shutdown` and the no-window timer mean "close this server".
+   The host exits when the last server closes and 30 min pass idle (the packaged watchdog moves to the host).
+3. **A crash in a server** (an uncaught error raised while handling that server's work) is logged into that window,
+   every server flushes, and the host keeps running; five uncaught errors inside a minute, or one with no server to
+   pin it on, flush everything and exit 1 (the manager restarts the host; windows reload their projects).
+4. **Updates and restarts** load a new build only when the host restarts, which restarts every window. The
+   manager's per-window "restart" becomes stop + start of that window's server (same build).
+5. **Demos** stay a child process of their own (their mock adapter and `_demoMode` must not reach real windows).
+
+## 3. One priority queue for every model call (`src/llm/scheduler.mjs`, replaces token-budget.mjs)
+
+Every call from every window, sub-agents and the Inspector included, asks the scheduler before it goes
+(`await scheduler.admit({ level, model, tokens, who, server, signal })`, in the caller's own async context - no
+deferred callbacks, so AsyncLocalStorage contexts stay intact) and reports what it actually used when it ends.
+
+| Level | Who |
+|---|---|
+| 0 | the main agent (and side chat, compaction, the history helper a snippet asks), and anything the developer is waiting on: Inspector chat, probes, test connection |
+| 1 | the bookkeeper |
+| 2 | sub-agents (the review checker included) |
+| 3 | Heads up, the dev check, the skills maintainer, recall, commit suggestions - the rest |
+
+- **Limits per model:** tokens a minute (Settings, else known, else learned from a 429), requests a minute (Settings,
+  else learned from a 429), and at most `CB_LLM_BACKGROUND_INFLIGHT` (6) background calls in flight. Level 0 never
+  waits for any of them; it only counts.
+- **Counted exactly:** what each call sends when it goes, plus its completion tokens from usage when it ends (a hedge's
+  second copy and a retry count too). One process, so the count is every window's.
+- **Order and pressure:** waiting calls go highest level first, oldest first within a level, round-robin across
+  windows. A level-L call may go while the minute's use plus its tokens leaves the share reserved for the levels above
+  it: `reserve(L) = limit x 0.4 x (L / 3) x max(0, 1 - waited / patience(L))` - the reserve melts as it waits; past
+  `maxWait(L)` it goes whatever the count. Patience 30 / 60 / 90 s and max wait 60 / 120 / 180 s for levels 1 / 2 / 3.
+- **A 429** (any level) holds levels 1-3 for that model until its Retry-After, as today, and teaches a limit when the
+  model has none.
+- The gateway's `x-ratelimit-remaining-tokens-minute` is kept as a check: when it says less is left than the count
+  does (other users of the key), the count is raised to match.
+
+## 4. The bookkeeper runs whenever something is unprocessed
+
+Today: at run end, every 10 exchanges mid-run, or on `<bookkeeper/>`. Now: whenever a chat has stream items past
+`st.marked`, a pass is requested after a 3 s settle (a burst becomes one pass), on the existing one-at-a-time chain
+(`_bkChain`); when a pass ends and more arrived meanwhile, another is requested. The end-of-run pass (with its
+end-of-run ask) still runs at run end. Heads up stays after the run, at level 3. `MID_RUN_EVERY` goes.
+
+## 5. Global resources
+
+- **Masking workers:** one pool for the process (min(4, cores - 1) threads), every text masked off the main thread
+  (`FILTER_WORKER_MIN` 0 for a worker filter), unmasking too, ordered by the caller's level.
+- **Sub-agents:** `SUBAGENT_MAX_RUNNING` counts across the process.
+- **Disk:** one append queue per log file (raw log, diag, project log, turn log); the project scan one at a time.
+- **One plugin manager, filter pipeline and token store** for the process (fixes plugins' `init(host)` running N times
+  on one module instance).
+
+## 6. Off the event loop (async sweep), in order of what stalls six windows most
+
+1. Conversation save: no full rewrite for a `rawId` stamp (stamped before the flush, or written as an appended patch
+   line); writes through one async queue per chat; a sync flush kept only for exit.
+2. Raw log: stringified once, appended through the queue; rotation in the queue.
+3. Masking and unmasking on the pool (5); piishape's own file reads (`existsSync` probes, pack `.idx`, the index
+   flush, `recoverFromOrigins`) moved into the worker or made async.
+4. Snippet file helpers (`countLinesStreaming`, `readLineSlice`, `open()`/`at()`, `realResolve`), `zipRead`'s inflate,
+   the docx compile: async.
+5. Per-request settings stat and `loadPrefs`: from memory (the settings bus); logs through the append queue (a sync
+   write kept for the crash path).
+6. The project scan (`walkProbes`): async, yielding between folders.
+7. Vault local mode: in memory with one writer (no walk, no busy spin).
+
+## 7. Using the single process
+
+- Settings changes reach every window at once (an event bus) - the 2 s stamp poll goes.
+- The vault and issued tokens are read once and kept in memory.
+- One help chat for every window.
+- One held-watch thread and one stall timer, reporting which windows were busy.
+- A new window opens in the time it takes to bind a port.
+
+## 8. Stages (each: tests, commit, push, build)
+
+1. Host + window servers in one process, per-server lifecycle, the A-class fixes (lock, timers, singletons with one
+   handler, env -> options, one plugin manager), `serve` and `host` entry points, launch-cmd.
+2. The scheduler; every call site states its level.
+3. The bookkeeper on unprocessed items.
+4. The Java manager on the host.
+5. The async sweep (6), then the resource pool (5).
+6. Single-process uses (7).
+

+// (enum ... sessionid, 2026-11-18, prod: an agent reading code_boss's plugin-authoring documentation and a browser

+  // ── the host: one node process for every instance (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md) ──

+  /** What the host's life needs of the machine - the container, its bridge, its ports. The container's own, or a test's. */
+  interface Io {
+    /** The container's drive letter; refused when the container is not running. */
+    String drive() throws Sandbox.Failure;
+    /** Start `node code-boss.mjs host` inside, with these variables over the container's environment, its output into .cb-run\code-boss-host.txt. */
+    HostProc startHost(Map<String, String> env, String drive) throws Sandbox.Failure;
+    /** The host's control port, published for this manager alone: the port out here. */
+    int publishHost(int insidePort) throws Sandbox.Failure;
+    void publish(int outsidePort, int insidePort) throws Sandbox.Failure;
+    void unpublish(int outsidePort);
+    Container.Cleared clearPort(int port, int sparePid);
+  }
+  interface HostProc { Integer waitExit(int millis); void terminateTree(); void close(); }
+
+  static final class ContainerIo implements Io {
+    public String drive() throws Sandbox.Failure {
+      Sandbox s; String z;
+      synchronized (Container.LOCK) { s = Container.sb; z = Container.drive; }
+      if (s == null || z.isEmpty() || !"running".equals(Container.state)) throw new Sandbox.Failure("The container is not running, so code_boss was not started.");
+      return z;
+    }
+    public HostProc startHost(Map<String, String> vars, String z) throws Sandbox.Failure {
+      Sandbox s;
+      synchronized (Container.LOCK) { s = Container.sb; }
+      if (s == null) throw new Sandbox.Failure("The container stopped before code_boss was started.");
+      Map<String, String> env = new LinkedHashMap<String, String>(Container.environment(z, Container.defaultJdk()));
+      for (Map.Entry<String, String> e : vars.entrySet()) Server.put(env, e.getKey(), e.getValue());
+      String inner = "\"" + z + "\\tools\\node\\node.exe\" \"" + z + "\\tools\\app\\code-boss.mjs\" host > \"" + z + "\\.cb-run\\" + HOST_OUT + "\" 2>&1";
+      final Sandbox.Proc p = s.startWithEnvironment("\"" + System.getenv("SystemRoot") + "\\System32\\cmd.exe\" /c \"" + inner + "\"", z + "\\projects", env);
+      return new HostProc() {
+        public Integer waitExit(int millis) { return p.waitExit(millis); }
+        public void terminateTree() { p.terminateTree(); }
+        public void close() { p.close(); }
+      };
+    }
+    public int publishHost(int insidePort) throws Sandbox.Failure { return Container.publishAny(insidePort); }
+    public void publish(int outsidePort, int insidePort) throws Sandbox.Failure { Container.publish(outsidePort, insidePort); }
+    public void unpublish(int outsidePort) { Container.unpublish(outsidePort); }
+    public Container.Cleared clearPort(int port, int sparePid) { return Container.clearPort(port, sparePid); }
+  }
+  static volatile Io io = new ContainerIo();
+
+  static final class Host {
+    volatile String state = "stopped";   // stopped | starting | running | stopping | failed
+    volatile HostProc proc;
+    volatile String key = "", drive = "", build = "", error = "", detail = "", lastLine = "", restartWhy = "";
+    volatile int outside, pid, gen, restarts, starts;
+    volatile long startedAt, since, answeredAt, restartedAt;
+    volatile boolean stopAsked;
+    final List<Long> crashes = new ArrayList<Long>();
+  }
+  static final Host HOST = new Host();
+  /** One start, restart or stop of the host at a time. Never taken while LOCK is held. */
+  static final Object HOST_LOCK = new Object();
+
+  static Map<String, Object> hostBrief() {
+    Map<String, Object> m = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+    String s = HOST.state;
+    m.put("state", s);
+    m.put("detail", HOST.detail);
+    m.put("error", HOST.error);
+    m.put("pid", Integer.valueOf(HOST.pid));
+    m.put("build", HOST.build);
+    m.put("insidePort", Integer.valueOf(HOST_INSIDE_PORT));
+    m.put("controlPort", Integer.valueOf(HOST.outside));
+    m.put("since", Long.valueOf("running".equals(s) ? HOST.since : 0));
+    long silent = "running".equals(s) && HOST.answeredAt > 0 ? System.currentTimeMillis() - HOST.answeredAt : 0;
+    m.put("silentMs", Long.valueOf(silent));
+    m.put("starts", Integer.valueOf(HOST.starts));
+    m.put("restarts", Integer.valueOf(HOST.restarts));
+    m.put("restartWhy", HOST.restartWhy);
+    m.put("restartedAt", Long.valueOf(HOST.restartedAt));
+    m.put("output", ".cb-run\\" + HOST_OUT);
+    return m;
+  }
+
+  static boolean hostAnswering() { return "running".equals(HOST.state) && HOST.answeredAt > 0 && System.currentTimeMillis() - HOST.answeredAt < 6000L; }
+
+  /** How long a host that is starting is waited for before it is given up on: 5 min. */
+  static long hostStartMs() { return 300000L; }
+
+  /** The host, up and answering: started if it is not. */
+  static void ensureHost() throws Sandbox.Failure {
+    synchronized (HOST_LOCK) {
+      HostProc p = HOST.proc;
+      if ("running".equals(HOST.state) && p != null && p.waitExit(0) == null) return;
+      startHostLocked();
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** Start the host (HOST_LOCK held): a new key, its control port cleared and published, `node code-boss.mjs host`, waited for until /host/health answers. */
+  private static void startHostLocked() throws Sandbox.Failure {
+    final int gen = ++HOST.gen;
+    HOST.state = "starting"; HOST.error = ""; HOST.detail = "Starting code_boss"; HOST.lastLine = ""; HOST.pid = 0; HOST.build = "";
+    HOST.startedAt = System.currentTimeMillis(); HOST.answeredAt = 0; HOST.since = 0;
+    HOST.key = randomKey();   // made here, for this start only, and kept in this manager's memory alone (never in a file)
+    HostProc p = null;
+    try {
+      String z = io.drive();
+      HOST.drive = z;
+      if (HOST.stopAsked) throw new Sandbox.Failure("The container is stopping.");
+      // a code_boss LEFT RUNNING in the container on its control port (an earlier manager's host) is ended first; anything outside the container is never touched
+      Container.Cleared held = io.clearPort(HOST_INSIDE_PORT, 0);
+      if (!held.free) throw new Sandbox.Failure("Port " + HOST_INSIDE_PORT + " is already in use on this machine. It is the port code_boss listens on for this manager, inside the container - and the container shares its port numbers with the rest of the machine. " + held.said + " Whatever has it has to go first. Nothing was started.");
+      HOST.detail = "Opening the way in for the container manager";
+      HOST.outside = io.publishHost(HOST_INSIDE_PORT);
+      // WHAT THE RUN BEFORE PRINTED IS KEPT (prod, 2026-11-15: a code_boss restarted for not answering, and what it had
+      // printed - where a crash leaves its last words - was deleted as the next one started): beside it, as .prev
+      Path out = hostOut(), prevOut = out.resolveSibling(HOST_OUT + ".prev");
+      try { Files.createDirectories(out.getParent()); if (Files.exists(out)) Files.move(out, prevOut, java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING); }
+      catch (IOException e) { try { Files.deleteIfExists(out); } catch (IOException ignore) { } }
+      Map<String, String> env = new LinkedHashMap<String, String>();
+      env.put("CB_HOST_PORT", String.valueOf(HOST_INSIDE_PORT));
+      env.put("CB_HOST_KEY", HOST.key);
+      env.put("CB_HOME", z + "\\home");
+      env.put("CB_DEFAULT_FOLDER", z + "\\projects");
+      env.put("CB_IN_CONTAINER", "1");
+      env.put("CB_CONTAINER_API", "http://127.0.0.1:" + Api.INSIDE_PORT);   // what the container offers the agent and its plugins (Api)
+      env.put("CB_CONTAINER_TOKEN", Api.token == null ? "" : Api.token);
+      // (where this run's output goes and the run before's was kept: code_boss writes the run before's last lines to its
+      // diag.log as it starts, and its inspector reads them)
+      env.put("CB_RUN_OUT", z + "\\.cb-run\\" + HOST_OUT);
+      if (Files.exists(prevOut)) env.put("CB_RUN_OUT_PREV", z + "\\.cb-run\\" + prevOut.getFileName());
+      env.put("OPEN_BROWSER", "0");   // it must not try to open a browser from in there: the developer's windows are opened out here
+      if (HOST.stopAsked) throw new Sandbox.Failure("The container is stopping.");
+      HOST.detail = "Starting code_boss inside the container";
+      p = io.startHost(env, z);
+      HOST.proc = p;
+      HOST.starts++;
+      long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + hostStartMs();
+      while (true) {
+        if (HOST.stopAsked) throw new Sandbox.Failure("The container is stopping.");
+        Integer exit = p.waitExit(700);
+        String txt = readTail(out, 65536);
+        HOST.lastLine = Server.lastLine(txt);
+        if (exit != null) throw new Sandbox.Failure("code_boss ended before it was answering (exit " + exit + "). " + (txt.trim().isEmpty() ? "It said nothing." : "The end of what it said:\n" + Server.tail(txt, 14)));
+        if (askHealth() != null) break;
+        if (System.currentTimeMillis() > until) throw new Sandbox.Failure("code_boss did not answer within " + Domain.took(hostStartMs()) + " of being started." + (txt.trim().isEmpty() ? " It said nothing." : " The end of what it said:\n" + Server.tail(txt, 14)));
+      }
+      HOST.since = System.currentTimeMillis(); HOST.detail = ""; HOST.lastLine = ""; HOST.state = "running";
+    } catch (Sandbox.Failure f) {
+      endHostProc(p, "it did not start");
+      HOST.proc = null;
+      if (HOST.outside > 0) io.unpublish(HOST.outside);
+      HOST.outside = 0; HOST.pid = 0;
+      HOST.state = "failed"; HOST.error = f.getMessage(); HOST.detail = "";
+      throw f;
+    }
+    Thread t = new Thread(new Runnable() { public void run() { watch(gen); } }, "cb-host-watch-" + gen);
+    t.setDaemon(true);
+    t.start();
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * THE HOST'S WATCH, one thread for each start of it: is it still there, and answering? Every 2 s its /host/health -
+   * each server's windows, project and status, for its instance. Silent for AUTO_RESTART_MS: restarted (every window
+   * with it - asked once more first, so a restart never rests on a stale answer). Ended by itself while windows were on
+   * it: started again (a crash - ONE-PROCESS-SPEC 2.3); ended with exit 0: its windows are stopped.
+   */
+  private static void watch(int gen) {
+    int tick = 0;
+    long loopAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
+    while (HOST.gen == gen) {
+      HostProc p = HOST.proc;
+      if (p == null) return;
+      Integer exit = p.waitExit(1000);
+      if (HOST.gen != gen) return;
+      // THE MACHINE SLEPT (a turn of this loop took far longer than its second): nothing was asked meanwhile, so the
+      // time since the last answer is not silence - it is counted again from now (the developer's side agent,
+      // 2026-11-06: a restart just after waking would stop whatever code_boss was doing)
+      long now0 = System.currentTimeMillis();
+      if (now0 - loopAt > 30000L && HOST.answeredAt > 0) { HOST.answeredAt = now0; synchronized (LOCK) { for (Inst i : ALL.values()) if (i.registered()) i.answeredAt = now0; } }
+      loopAt = now0;
+      if (exit != null) { hostEnded(gen, exit.intValue()); return; }
+      if (++tick % 2 == 0) look(gen);
+      long quiet = HOST.answeredAt > 0 ? System.currentTimeMillis() - HOST.answeredAt : 0;
+      if (quiet >= autoRestartMs()) {
+        look(gen);
+        quiet = HOST.answeredAt > 0 ? System.currentTimeMillis() - HOST.answeredAt : 0;
+        if (HOST.gen == gen && quiet >= autoRestartMs()) {
+          String why = "it had not answered for " + Domain.took(quiet);
+          if (registeredCount() == 0) stopHost(gen, why); else restartHost(gen, why);
+          return;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  private static int registeredCount() {
+    int n = 0;
+    synchronized (LOCK) { for (Inst i : ALL.values()) if (i.registered() && !i.stopAsked) n++; }
+    return n;
+  }
+
+  /** The host's /host/health, through the bridge; null when it did not answer. An answer is the host answering. */
+  static Map<String, Object> askHealth() {
+    try {
+      Answer a = hostCall("GET", "/host/health", null, 2500);
+      if (a.code != 200) return null;
+      HOST.answeredAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
+      HOST.pid = Json.integer(a.json.get("pid"));
+      HOST.build = Json.string(a.json.get("build"));
+      return a.json;
+    } catch (IOException e) { return null; }
+  }
+
+  /** Ask the host how each window's server is: windows showing it, its project. A miss changes nothing; a server it no longer lists closed itself. */
+  private static void look(int gen) {
+    long asked = System.currentTimeMillis();
+    Map<String, Object> h = askHealth();
+    if (h == null || HOST.gen != gen) return;
+    Map<String, Map<String, Object>> byId = new LinkedHashMap<String, Map<String, Object>>();
+    for (Object o : Json.array(h.get("servers"))) { Map<String, Object> s = Json.object(o); byId.put(Json.string(s.get("id")), s); }
+    boolean starting = false;
+    synchronized (LOCK) {
+      for (Inst i : ALL.values()) {
+        if ("starting".equals(i.state)) starting = true;
+        if (!i.registered()) continue;
+        Map<String, Object> s = byId.get(i.serverId);
+        if (s != null) {
+          i.answeredAt = HOST.answeredAt;
+          i.windows = Json.integer(s.get("windows"));
+          i.project = Json.string(s.get("project"));
+          i.serverStatus = Json.string(s.get("status"));
+        } else if (i.registeredAt < asked && "running".equals(i.state) && !i.hostRestarting && !i.stopAsked) {
+          i.gone = true;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    if (starting) HOST.lastLine = Server.lastLine(readTail(hostOut(), 65536));
+  }
+
+  /** It ended by itself. Exit 0 (it quit, or had nothing left to serve): its windows are stopped. Otherwise, with windows on it: started again. */
+  private static void hostEnded(int gen, int exit) {
+    String said = readTail(hostOut(), 65536);
+    List<Inst> back = new ArrayList<Inst>();
+    synchronized (HOST_LOCK) {
+      if (HOST.gen != gen || HOST.stopAsked) return;
+      HostProc p = HOST.proc;
+      synchronized (LOCK) { for (Inst i : ALL.values()) if (i.registered() && !i.stopAsked) back.add(i); }
+      if (exit == 0 || back.isEmpty()) {
+        HOST.gen++;
+        HOST.proc = null;
+        try { if (p != null) p.close(); } catch (Throwable ignore) { }
+        if (HOST.outside > 0) io.unpublish(HOST.outside);
+        HOST.outside = 0; HOST.pid = 0;
+        HOST.state = exit == 0 ? "stopped" : "failed"; HOST.detail = "";
+        HOST.error = exit == 0 ? "" : "code_boss ended by itself (exit " + exit + "). " + (said.trim().isEmpty() ? "It said nothing." : "The end of what it said:\n" + Server.tail(said, 14));
+        synchronized (LOCK) { for (Inst i : back) i.gone = true; }
+        return;
+      }
+      // A CRASH, again and again: started again only so often - then its windows say why
+      long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
+      synchronized (HOST.crashes) {
+        for (java.util.Iterator<Long> it = HOST.crashes.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) if (now - it.next().longValue() > 600000L) it.remove();
+        if (HOST.crashes.size() >= 3) {
+          HOST.gen++;
+          HOST.proc = null;
+          try { if (p != null) p.close(); } catch (Throwable ignore) { }
+          if (HOST.outside > 0) io.unpublish(HOST.outside);
+          HOST.outside = 0; HOST.pid = 0;
+          String why = "code_boss ended by itself (exit " + exit + "), for the fourth time in ten minutes, and was not started again. " + (said.trim().isEmpty() ? "It said nothing." : "The end of what it said:\n" + Server.tail(said, 14));
+          HOST.state = "failed"; HOST.error = why; HOST.detail = "";
+          synchronized (LOCK) { for (Inst i : back) { i.failWhy = why; i.serverId = ""; i.stopAsked = true; } }
+          return;
+        }
+        HOST.crashes.add(Long.valueOf(now));
+      }
+    }
+    restartHost(gen, "it ended by itself (exit " + exit + ")");
+  }
+
+  /** Restart the host on a thread of its own (asked for from a window or the page). */
+  static void restartHostSoon(final int gen, final String why) {
+    Thread t = new Thread(new Runnable() { public void run() { restartHost(gen, why); } }, "cb-host-restart");
+    t.setDaemon(true);
+    t.start();
+  }
+
+  /** The page's "restart code_boss" for every window at once: the host restarted (a new build is loaded only so). */
+  static Map<String, Object> restartHost(String why) {
+    if (!"running".equals(HOST.state)) return answer("code_boss is not running.");
+    restartHostSoon(HOST.gen, why);
+    return answer(null);
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * RESTART THE HOST - every window: the one that is there ended (asked to quit; ended outright after a few seconds),
+   * its control port and every window's port cleared, a new one started, and each window that was running asked of it
+   * again, with why. Its windows stay open and reconnect. Only for the start of the host it was asked about (gen).
+   */
+  static void restartHost(int gen, String why) {
+    List<Inst> back = new ArrayList<Inst>();
+    synchronized (HOST_LOCK) {
+      if (HOST.gen != gen || HOST.stopAsked) return;
+      synchronized (LOCK) {
+        for (Inst i : ALL.values()) {
+          if (("running".equals(i.state) || "starting".equals(i.state)) && !i.stopAsked && i.registered()) {
+            back.add(i); i.hostRestarting = true; i.restartWhy = why; i.detail = "Restarting code_boss: " + why;
+          }
+        }
+      }
+      HOST.state = "stopping"; HOST.detail = "Restarting code_boss: " + why; HOST.restartWhy = why;
+      HostProc old = HOST.proc;
+      HOST.gen++;   // the old watch, and every server asked of the old host, are done
+      endHostProc(old, "restarted: " + why);
+      HOST.proc = null;
+      io.clearPort(HOST_INSIDE_PORT, 0);
+      for (int n = 1; n <= MAX; n++) io.clearPort(INSIDE_PORT + n - 1, 0);
+      try { startHostLocked(); }
+      catch (Sandbox.Failure f) {
+        // (the container stopping meanwhile is no failure: its instances are stopping with it)
+        synchronized (LOCK) { for (Inst i : back) { if (!HOST.stopAsked && !i.stopAsked) i.failWhy = "It was stopped to be restarted (" + why + "), and could not be started again: " + f.getMessage(); i.serverId = ""; i.hostRestarting = false; i.stopAsked = true; } }
+        return;
+      }
+      HOST.restarts++; HOST.restartedAt = System.currentTimeMillis();
+      String stamp = stamped(why);
+      for (Inst i : back) {
+        try {
+          if (i.stopAsked) continue;
+          register(i, stamp);
+          synchronized (LOCK) { i.restarts++; i.restartedAt = System.currentTimeMillis(); if ("running".equals(i.state)) i.detail = ""; }
+        } catch (Exception e) {
+          synchronized (LOCK) { if (!HOST.stopAsked && !i.stopAsked) i.failWhy = "It was restarted with code_boss (" + why + "), and its server could not be started again: " + e.getMessage(); i.serverId = ""; i.stopAsked = true; }
+        } finally {
+          i.hostRestarting = false;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** The host stopped: asked to quit (every server flushed), ended outright after 8 s. Its windows' servers go with it. */
+  static void stopHost(String why) { stopHost(-1, why); }
+
+  private static void stopHost(int gen, String why) {
+    if (gen < 0) HOST.stopAsked = true;   // a host still starting gives up at once
+    synchronized (HOST_LOCK) {
+      try {
+        if (gen >= 0 && HOST.gen != gen) return;
+        HostProc p = HOST.proc;
+        HOST.gen++;
+        HOST.proc = null;
+        if (p != null) { HOST.state = "stopping"; HOST.detail = "Stopping code_boss: " + why; endHostProc(p, why); }
+        if (HOST.outside > 0) io.unpublish(HOST.outside);
+        HOST.outside = 0; HOST.pid = 0;
+        HOST.state = "stopped"; HOST.error = ""; HOST.detail = "";
+      } finally {
+        if (gen < 0) HOST.stopAsked = false;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** Ask it to quit (POST /host/quit: every server flushed), wait 8 s, then end its process tree outright. */
+  private static void endHostProc(HostProc p, String why) {
+    if (p == null) return;
+    try {
+      if (p.waitExit(0) == null) {
+        Map<String, Object> none = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        try { hostCall("POST", "/host/quit?why=" + enc(why), none, 2500); } catch (IOException notAnswered) { }
+        p.waitExit(8000);
+      }
+    } catch (Throwable ignore) { }
+    try { if (p.waitExit(0) == null) { p.terminateTree(); p.waitExit(10000); } } catch (Throwable ignore) { }
+    try { p.close(); } catch (Throwable ignore) { }
+  }
+
+  static final class Answer { int code; String text = ""; Map<String, Object> json = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>(); }
+
+  /** One request to the host's control API, through the bridge, with its key. IOException: it did not answer. */
+  static Answer hostCall(String method, String pathAndQuery, Object body, int readMs) throws IOException {
+    int port = HOST.outside;
+    String key = HOST.key;
+    if (port <= 0) throw new IOException("code_boss is not started");
+    HttpURLConnection c = (HttpURLConnection) new URL("http://127.0.0.1:" + port + pathAndQuery).openConnection(Proxy.NO_PROXY);
+    try {
+      c.setConnectTimeout(1500);
+      c.setReadTimeout(readMs);
+      c.setRequestMethod(method);
+      c.setRequestProperty("X-CB-Host-Key", key);
+      if (body != null) {
+        byte[] b = Json.write(body).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+        c.setDoOutput(true);
+        c.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json");
+        c.setFixedLengthStreamingMode(b.length);
+        try (java.io.OutputStream o = c.getOutputStream()) { o.write(b); }
+      }
+      Answer a = new Answer();
+      a.code = c.getResponseCode();
+      java.io.InputStream in = a.code >= 400 ? c.getErrorStream() : c.getInputStream();
+      if (in != null) {
+        java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream out = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
+        byte[] buf = new byte[4096];
+        try { for (int n; (n = in.read(buf)) > 0 && out.size() < 1048576; ) out.write(buf, 0, n); } finally { in.close(); }
+        a.text = new String(out.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+        try { a.json = Json.object(Json.parse(a.text)); } catch (IllegalArgumentException notJson) { }
+      }
+      return a;
+    } finally {
+      c.disconnect();
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** The end of a file (what the host printed), at most `max` bytes. "" when there is none. */
+  static String readTail(Path p, int max) {
+    try (java.io.RandomAccessFile f = new java.io.RandomAccessFile(p.toFile(), "r")) {
+      long len = f.length(), from = Math.max(0, len - max);
+      byte[] b = new byte[(int) (len - from)];
+      f.seek(from);
+      f.readFully(b);
+      return new String(b, StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1);
+    } catch (IOException e) { return ""; }
+  }

+/**
+ * Walk `root` once for all `probes`. Resolves { hits: Map(probeName → [{ path, name }] sorted by path), visited,
+ * errors: [{ probe, path, error }] } — a probe that threw appears in `errors` and its hits so far are kept.
+ * WITHOUT HOLDING THE THREAD (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md section 6.6: one process hosts every window): each directory is
+ * read with fs/promises, so the walk lets go between directories; it goes in the same order as walkProbesSync, so it
+ * visits the same directories, within the same budget, with the same hits. One walk at a time in the process.

+let _dsQueue = Promise.resolve();
+export function walkProbes(root, probes, opts = {}) {
+  const run = _dsQueue.then(() => dsWalkAsync(root, probes, opts));
+  _dsQueue = run.catch(() => {});

+async function dsWalkAsync(root, probes, { maxDepth = DEFAULT_MAX_DEPTH, budget = DEFAULT_DIR_BUDGET, skipDirs = DEFAULT_SKIP_DIRS } = {}) {
+  const st = dsWalkState(probes);
+  if (!root || !st.list.length) return dsResult(st);
+  try { await stat(root); } catch { return dsResult(st); }
+  let left = budget;
+  const walk = async (dir, depth, active) => {
+    if (depth > maxDepth || left-- <= 0) return;
+    let entries;
+    try { entries = await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
+    const next = dsVisitDir(st, dir, entries, depth, active);
+    if (!next.length) return;   // nobody cares below here
+    for (const name of dsSubdirs(entries, skipDirs)) await walk(path.join(dir, name), depth + 1, next);
+  };
+  await walk(root, 0, st.list);
+  return dsResult(st);
+}

+const dxPathExists = (p) => stat(p).then(() => true, () => false);
+
+// A program run to its end, as spawnSync reports it ({ status, stdout, stderr, error }) - without holding the event
+// loop (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC 6.4: every window's server shares it, and a first compile takes seconds). Past timeoutMs it is
+// killed and error is ETIMEDOUT, as spawnSync's is.
+function dxRunProc(cmd, args, timeoutMs) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    let stdout = '', stderr = '', done = false, timedOut = false, child;
+    const finish = (r) => { if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ stdout, stderr, ...r }); };
+    try { child = spawn(cmd, args, { windowsHide: true, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'] }); } catch (e) { resolve({ status: null, stdout, stderr, error: e }); return; }
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => { timedOut = true; try { child.kill(); } catch {} }, timeoutMs);
+    child.stdout.setEncoding('utf8'); child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { stdout += d; });
+    child.stderr.setEncoding('utf8'); child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { stderr += d; });
+    child.on('error', (e) => finish({ status: null, error: e }));
+    child.on('close', (code) => finish(timedOut ? { status: null, error: Object.assign(new Error(`spawnSync ${cmd} ETIMEDOUT`), { code: 'ETIMEDOUT' }) } : { status: code }));
+    try { child.stdin.end(); } catch {}
+  });
+}

+const OWN_MASKED_COPIES = new Map();   // normalized absolute path -> { hash, statKey }
+const STEP_READS_FILE = new Set(['read', 'read_file', 'readJson', 'readCsv', 'open', 'streamNext', 'streamAt', 'search']);   // the snippet functions that hand over one file's text
+const ownMaskedCopyKey = (abs) => { const r = path.resolve(String(abs)); return process.platform === 'win32' ? r.toLowerCase() : r; };
+function registerOwnMaskedCopy(abs, body) {
+  try {
+    const k = ownMaskedCopyKey(abs);
+    OWN_MASKED_COPIES.delete(k);
+    OWN_MASKED_COPIES.set(k, { hash: createHash('sha256').update(String(body), 'utf8').digest('hex'), statKey: null });
+    while (OWN_MASKED_COPIES.size > 4000) OWN_MASKED_COPIES.delete(OWN_MASKED_COPIES.keys().next().value);
+  } catch { /* not registered: a read of it is masked like any file */ }
+}
+function forgetOwnMaskedCopy(abs) { try { OWN_MASKED_COPIES.delete(ownMaskedCopyKey(abs)); } catch {} }
+/** Whether `abs` is a copy code_boss wrote masked and nobody has changed since (its bytes are the ones written). */
+async function isOwnMaskedCopy(abs) {
+  try {
+    const e = OWN_MASKED_COPIES.get(ownMaskedCopyKey(abs));
+    if (!e) return false;
+    const st = await stat(abs);
+    if (!st.isFile()) return false;
+    const sk = `${st.size}:${st.mtimeMs}:${st.ctimeMs}:${st.ino}`;
+    if (e.statKey === sk) return true;
+    if (st.size > 256 * 1024 * 1024) return false;
+    if (createHash('sha256').update(await readFile(abs)).digest('hex') !== e.hash) return false;
+    e.statKey = sk;
+    return true;
+  } catch { return false; }

+/**
+ * THE AGENT MARKS A TODO (the developer, 2026-11-19: "make it where the main agent marks the checklist items as done
+ * instead of the bookkeeper. It's a small task and will make the checklist real time"): todo(id, 'doing' | 'done') in
+ * its snippet. Doing is the agent's own one: another the agent was doing (waiting on nothing) goes back to planned.
+ * `through`: the newest item of the conversation, the todo's place in time, as a pass sets it. Returns { text } to give
+ * back to the agent, or throws with what is wrong. The todo carries `byAgent` - the bookkeeper does not undo it.
+ */
+export function streamAgentTodo(st, id, state, { through = null } = {}) {
+  const n = Number(String(id ?? '').replace(/^\[|\]$/g, '').trim());
+  const t = Number.isInteger(n) ? st.todos.find((x) => x.id === n) : null;
+  const s = String(state == null ? 'done' : state).trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (s !== 'doing' && s !== 'done') throw new Error(`todo(${id}, "${state}"): the state is "doing" or "done" - waiting, held and dropped are the bookkeeper's`);
+  if (!t) {
+    const open = st.todos.filter((x) => TODO_OPEN.has(x.state)).map((x) => x.id);
+    throw new Error(`todo(${id}): no todo ${id}${open.length ? ` - the open ones are ${open.slice(0, 12).join(', ')}` : ' - none is open'}. The todos are numbered under "What's open" in your live state; a new one is written a moment after the work it is for comes up.`);
+  }
+  if (t.state === 'dropped') throw new Error(`todo ${t.id} was dropped${t.why ? ` (${t.why})` : ''}: it is not to be done`);
+  const was = t.state;
+  const moved = [];
+  if (s === 'doing') {
+    for (const o of st.todos) if (o !== t && o.state === 'doing' && !o.waits) { o.state = 'planned'; moved.push(o.id); }
+    t.state = 'doing';
+  } else { t.state = 'done'; delete t.waits; if (through) t.after = through; }
+  t.byAgent = s;
+  if (through) t.seen = through;
+  if (was !== t.state || moved.length) {
+    st.changes.push({ seq: st.seq + 1, what: `todo ${t.id}: ${was} -> ${t.state} (the agent)${moved.length ? `; ${moved.join(', ')} planned again` : ''}`, said: 'the agent' });
+    st.seq++;
+    st.changes = st.changes.slice(-STREAM_CHANGES_KEEP);
+  }
+  const next = s === 'done' ? st.todos.filter((x) => x.from === t.from && x.state === 'planned' && !x.waits).sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)[0] : null;
+  return { text: `todo ${t.id} ${t.state}: ${t.text}${moved.length ? ` (todo ${moved.join(', ')} planned again)` : ''}${next ? ` · next planned: [${next.id}] ${next.text}` : ''}` };
+}

=== add src/agent/zip-read.mjs
new 96d01c552bf32231d5264620
+/**
+ * zip-read.mjs - WHAT IS IN A JAR OR A ZIP, read in place: its entries, and one entry's bytes. No program run, nothing
+ * written: the archive's central directory is read, and an entry inflated (zlib) or copied (stored).
+ *
+ * (Found on the box, 2026-11-18: an agent wanted the classes in a dependency jar, to see how a router routes and checks
+ * messages; snippets had unzip (all of it, to a folder, through tar) and javap, nothing to look inside, so it wrote a
+ * node child script running jar.exe - which failed.)
+ *
+ *   zipEntries(buf)          -> [{ name, size, csize, method, offset, dir }]
+ *   zipEntry(buf, name)      -> Promise<Buffer> (the entry's bytes) | rejects when it is not there - async: a deflated
+ *                               entry inflates off the event loop (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC 6.4: every window shares it)
+ * ZIP64 archives (over 4 GB, or over 65,535 entries) are refused by name; encrypted entries too.
+ */
+import zlib from 'node:zlib';
+
+const EOCD_SIG = 0x06054b50, CEN_SIG = 0x02014b50, LOC_SIG = 0x04034b50;
+
+// zlib's inflateRaw as a promise (node:util is not in the bundle's header)
+function zrInflateRaw(raw) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => zlib.inflateRaw(raw, (err, out) => (err ? reject(err) : resolve(out))));
+}
+
+function zrEocd(buf) {
+  // the end-of-central-directory record: in the last 22 + 65,535 bytes (its comment may be up to 64 KB)
+  const stop = Math.max(0, buf.length - 22 - 0xffff);
+  for (let i = buf.length - 22; i >= stop; i--) if (buf.readUInt32LE(i) === EOCD_SIG) return i;
+  throw new Error('not a zip or jar (no end-of-central-directory record)');
+}
+
+export function zipEntries(buf) {
+  if (!Buffer.isBuffer(buf) || buf.length < 22) throw new Error('not a zip or jar (too short)');
+  const e = zrEocd(buf);
+  const count = buf.readUInt16LE(e + 10), size = buf.readUInt32LE(e + 12), start = buf.readUInt32LE(e + 16);
+  if (count === 0xffff || size === 0xffffffff || start === 0xffffffff) throw new Error('a ZIP64 archive (over 4 GB or 65,535 entries) - not read here; unzip it instead');
+  if (start + size > buf.length) throw new Error('not a zip or jar (its central directory is past its end)');
+  const out = [];
+  let p = start;
+  for (let k = 0; k < count; k++) {
+    if (p + 46 > buf.length || buf.readUInt32LE(p) !== CEN_SIG) throw new Error(`a broken zip: entry ${k + 1} of ${count} is not where its directory says`);
+    const flags = buf.readUInt16LE(p + 8), method = buf.readUInt16LE(p + 10);
+    const csize = buf.readUInt32LE(p + 20), usize = buf.readUInt32LE(p + 24);
+    const nlen = buf.readUInt16LE(p + 28), xlen = buf.readUInt16LE(p + 30), clen = buf.readUInt16LE(p + 32);
+    const offset = buf.readUInt32LE(p + 42);
+    const name = buf.toString(flags & 0x800 ? 'utf8' : 'latin1', p + 46, p + 46 + nlen);
+    out.push({ name, size: usize, csize, method, offset, dir: name.endsWith('/'), encrypted: !!(flags & 1) });
+    p += 46 + nlen + xlen + clen;
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+
+export async function zipEntry(buf, name, entries = null) {
+  const list = entries || zipEntries(buf);
+  const want = String(name || '').replace(/\\/g, '/').replace(/^\/+/, '');
+  const e = list.find((x) => x.name === want) || list.find((x) => x.name.toLowerCase() === want.toLowerCase());
+  if (!e) {
+    const like = list.filter((x) => !x.dir && x.name.toLowerCase().includes(want.toLowerCase().split('/').pop())).slice(0, 5).map((x) => x.name);
+    throw new Error(`"${want}" is not in the archive${like.length ? ` - did you mean: ${like.join(', ')}` : ''}`);
+  }
+  if (e.dir) throw new Error(`"${want}" is a folder in the archive, not a file`);
+  if (e.encrypted) throw new Error(`"${want}" is encrypted - not read here`);
+  const l = e.offset;
+  if (l + 30 > buf.length || buf.readUInt32LE(l) !== LOC_SIG) throw new Error(`a broken zip: "${want}" is not where its directory says`);
+  const data = l + 30 + buf.readUInt16LE(l + 26) + buf.readUInt16LE(l + 28);
+  const raw = buf.subarray(data, data + e.csize);
+  if (e.method === 0) return Buffer.from(raw);
+  if (e.method === 8) return zrInflateRaw(raw);
+  throw new Error(`"${want}" is packed with method ${e.method} (only stored and deflated are read here) - unzip it instead`);
+}
+

+    return existsSync(host) ? host : f;
=== add src/host-scope.mjs
new eb02ab6e085f38f9b08f9999
+// host-scope.mjs - WHICH WINDOW'S SERVER A PIECE OF WORK BELONGS TO (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md, 2026-11-18: one process
+// hosts a server per window). Every server's start, its timers and every request it serves run inside its scope, so
+// code shared by all of them - a module's handler, the LLM scheduler, a plugin's import - can tell whose work it is.
+//
+// What was per-process environment for a window (its port, instance number, browser port, window profile, the
+// manager's restart key, its run-output files) is the server's own `env` here: cbEnv(name) reads it, and falls back
+// to process.env, so a process with one server (tests, `serve`) reads what it always read.
+import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';
+
+const hsScope = new AsyncLocalStorage();
+let hsSeq = 0;
+
+/** A server's record: { id, env, port, onClose: [] } - the rest is the server's to add. */
+export function hsNewServer(env = {}) {
+  return { id: 's' + (++hsSeq), env: { ...(env || {}) }, port: null, onClose: [] };
+}
+/** Run `fn` as the work of `srv` (its start; a request it serves). */
+export function hsRun(srv, fn) { return hsScope.run(srv, fn); }
+/** The server whose work this is, or null (work started outside any server: a test calling a module directly). */
+export function hsCurrent() { return hsScope.getStore() || null; }
+/** The current server's id, or '' when there is none. */
+export function hsCurrentId() { const s = hsScope.getStore(); return (s && s.id) || ''; }
+
+// THE VALUES THAT WERE PER-PROCESS ENVIRONMENT FOR A WINDOW
+export const HS_WINDOW_ENV = ['PORT', 'CB_INSTANCE_ID', 'CB_BROWSER_PORT', 'CB_WINDOW_PROFILE', 'CB_WINDOW_HOST', 'CB_WINDOW_PORT',
+  'CB_RESTART_KEY', 'CB_MANAGER_URL', 'CB_RUN_OUT', 'CB_RUN_OUT_PREV', 'CB_RESTARTED_WHY', 'CB_LAUNCHER_ALIVE'];
+/** A window's value: its server's own, else the process environment's. */
+export function cbEnv(name) {
+  const s = hsScope.getStore();
+  if (s && s.env && s.env[name] != null) return s.env[name];
+  return process.env[name];
+}
+/** The current server's window values (for code that builds an environment object). */
+export function cbWindowEnv() {
+  const s = hsScope.getStore();
+  return s && s.env ? { ...s.env } : {};
+}

=== add src/host.mjs
new bf1cb1dd7deaacb2df4dca8b
+// host.mjs - THE ONE CODE_BOSS PROCESS (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md, the developer, 2026-11-18: "one process with multiple
+// servers ... have just one instance"). It holds a server per window - startServer(), each on its own port with its
+// own project - and answers whoever starts windows (the container's manager, the launcher) on a control port:
+//   GET    /host/health             { pid, build, uptime, servers: [{ id, port, project, windows, status, busy }] }
+//   POST   /host/servers            { port, instanceId?, browserPort?, windowProfile?, windowHost?, restartKey?, managerUrl?,
+//                                     runOut?, runOutPrev?, restartedWhy?, launcherAlive? } -> { id, port } once it listens
+//   DELETE /host/servers/<id>?why=  that window's server closes: its runs stopped, its conversation saved
+//   POST   /host/quit?why=          every server closes, then the process ends (exit 0)
+// Every call carries X-CB-Host-Key, the key its starter put in CB_HOST_KEY (taken out of the environment here, so no
+// process code_boss starts inherits it). With no server left for CB_HOST_IDLE_MS (30 min) the host ends by itself.
+import http from 'node:http';
+import { writeFileSync, unlinkSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const HOST_IDLE_MS_DEFAULT = 30 * 60 * 1000;
+const HOST_CLOSE_WAIT_MS = 8000;
+
+// the fields of POST /host/servers -> the window's values (what was per-process environment: host-scope.mjs cbEnv)
+const HOST_ENV_OF = {
+  port: 'PORT', instanceId: 'CB_INSTANCE_ID', browserPort: 'CB_BROWSER_PORT', windowProfile: 'CB_WINDOW_PROFILE',
+  windowHost: 'CB_WINDOW_HOST', windowPort: 'CB_WINDOW_PORT', restartKey: 'CB_RESTART_KEY', managerUrl: 'CB_MANAGER_URL',
+  runOut: 'CB_RUN_OUT', runOutPrev: 'CB_RUN_OUT_PREV', restartedWhy: 'CB_RESTARTED_WHY', launcherAlive: 'CB_LAUNCHER_ALIVE',
+};
+export function hostWindowEnv(body) {
+  const env = {};
+  for (const [k, name] of Object.entries(HOST_ENV_OF)) if (body && body[k] != null && body[k] !== '') env[name] = String(body[k]);
+  return env;
+}
+// (compared in constant time: every character looked at whatever the first difference)
+const hostKeyOk = (given, key) => {
+  const a = String(given || ''), b = String(key || '');
+  if (!b || a.length !== b.length) return false;
+  let d = 0;
+  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) d |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);
+  return d === 0;
+};
+
+/**
+ * Run the host. `startServer(opts)` and `setHostMode(on)` come from server.mjs; `serverOpts` is what every window's
+ * server is started with (html, baseURL, apiKey, defaultModel...). Resolves with { port, close } once the control
+ * port listens. `exit(code)` ends the process (injectable for tests).
+ */
+export async function runHost({ startServer, setHostMode, serverOpts = {}, controlPort, key = process.env.CB_HOST_KEY, build = '',
+  homeDir = null, idleMs = Number(process.env.CB_HOST_IDLE_MS) || HOST_IDLE_MS_DEFAULT, log = (m) => { try { process.stderr.write('[code_boss host] ' + m + '\n'); } catch {} },
+  exit = (code) => process.exit(code) } = {}) {
+  delete process.env.CB_HOST_KEY;
+  if (!key) throw new Error('the host needs a key (CB_HOST_KEY): its control port answers only the one who started it');
+  setHostMode(true);
+  const servers = new Map();   // id -> { id, port, server, session }
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  let idleTimer = null, quitting = false;
+  const registry = homeDir ? path.join(homeDir, '.code_boss', 'host.json') : null;
+
+  const idleCheck = () => {
+    if (idleTimer) { clearTimeout(idleTimer); idleTimer = null; }
+    if (servers.size || quitting || !(idleMs > 0)) return;
+    idleTimer = setTimeout(() => { if (!servers.size && !quitting) { log(`no window for ${Math.round(idleMs / 60000)} min: the host ends`); quit('idle'); } }, idleMs);
+    if (idleTimer.unref) idleTimer.unref();
+  };
+  const closeServer = (rec, why) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+    if (!rec || rec.closing) return resolve(rec && rec.closing);
+    log(`window server ${rec.id} (port ${rec.port}) closes: ${why}`);
+    rec.closing = true;
+    let done = false;
+    const end = () => { if (done) return; done = true; servers.delete(rec.id); idleCheck(); resolve(true); };
+    try { rec.server.close(end); } catch { end(); }
+    // (an open stream - a window's SSE - would hold close() until it ends: the server is going)
+    try { rec.server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {}
+    setTimeout(end, HOST_CLOSE_WAIT_MS).unref?.();
+  });
+  // A window's server stops as it always did - its runs stopped, the conversation saved (its own stopCodeBoss) - and
+  // then it is closed here instead of the process ending.
+  const stopServer = async (rec, why) => {
+    if (!rec || rec.closing) return;
+    try {
+      await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${rec.port}/api/shutdown`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ why }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(3000) });
+      // (its stopCodeBoss calls onStop -> closeServer; if it did not answer, it is closed here all the same)
+      for (let i = 0; i < 40 && servers.has(rec.id); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    } catch { /* it did not answer: closed below */ }
+    if (servers.has(rec.id)) await closeServer(rec, why);
+  };
+  const quit = async (why) => {
+    if (quitting) return;
+    quitting = true;
+    log(`quit (${why}): ${servers.size} window server(s) close`);
+    await Promise.all([...servers.values()].map((r) => stopServer(r, 'the host is quitting: ' + why)));
+    try { control.close(); } catch {}
+    try { if (registry) unlinkSync(registry); } catch {}
+    exit(0);
+  };
+  const startWindow = async (body) => {
+    const port = Number(body && body.port);
+    if (!(port > 0 && port < 65536)) throw Object.assign(new Error('a port is needed: { port }'), { status: 400 });
+    for (const r of servers.values()) if (r.port === port && !r.closing) throw Object.assign(new Error(`port ${port} already has window server ${r.id}`), { status: 409 });
+    let rec = null;
+    const started = await startServer({
+      ...serverOpts, port, windowEnv: hostWindowEnv({ ...body, port }),
+      onStop: (why) => { if (rec) closeServer(rec, why); },
+    });
+    rec = { id: started.id, port: started.port || port, server: started.server, session: started.session };
+    servers.set(rec.id, rec);
+    idleCheck();
+    log(`window server ${rec.id} listens on ${rec.port}`);
+    return rec;
+  };
+  const describe = (r) => {
+    let project = '', windows = 0, status = 'idle';
+    try { project = r.session.activeProject || ''; } catch {}
+    try { windows = r.session.streamSubscribers ? r.session.streamSubscribers.size : 0; } catch {}
+    try { status = r.session.status || 'idle'; } catch {}
+    return { id: r.id, port: r.port, project, windows, status, busy: status === 'running' };
+  };
+
+  const send = (res, code, obj) => { res.writeHead(code, { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'cache-control': 'no-store' }); res.end(JSON.stringify(obj)); };
+  const readJson = (req) => new Promise((resolve) => { let b = ''; req.setEncoding('utf8'); req.on('data', (c) => { if (b.length < 65536) b += c; }); req.on('end', () => { try { resolve(b ? JSON.parse(b) : {}); } catch { resolve(null); } }); req.on('error', () => resolve(null)); });
+  const control = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
+    try {
+      if (!hostKeyOk(req.headers['x-cb-host-key'], key)) return send(res, 403, { ok: false, error: 'the host answers only the one who started it (X-CB-Host-Key)' });
+      const u = new URL(req.url || '/', 'http://127.0.0.1');
+      const why = (u.searchParams.get('why') || '').slice(0, 120);
+      if (req.method === 'GET' && u.pathname === '/host/health') {
+        return send(res, 200, { ok: true, pid: process.pid, build, uptime: Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000), quitting, servers: [...servers.values()].filter((r) => !r.closing).map(describe) });
+      }
+      if (req.method === 'POST' && u.pathname === '/host/servers') {
+        if (quitting) return send(res, 409, { ok: false, error: 'the host is quitting' });
+        const body = await readJson(req);
+        if (!body) return send(res, 400, { ok: false, error: 'the body is not JSON' });
+        try { const r = await startWindow(body); return send(res, 200, { ok: true, id: r.id, port: r.port }); }
+        catch (e) { return send(res, e.status || 500, { ok: false, error: String(e.message || e), code: e.code || null }); }
+      }
+      const m = /^\/host\/servers\/([^/]+)$/.exec(u.pathname);
+      if (req.method === 'DELETE' && m) {
+        const rec = servers.get(decodeURIComponent(m[1]));
+        if (!rec) return send(res, 404, { ok: false, error: 'no window server ' + m[1] });
+        await stopServer(rec, why || 'its window was closed');
+        return send(res, 200, { ok: true, closed: rec.id });
+      }
+      if (req.method === 'POST' && u.pathname === '/host/quit') {
+        send(res, 200, { ok: true, quitting: true });
+        setTimeout(() => quit(why || 'asked to quit'), 20);
+        return;
+      }
+      return send(res, 404, { ok: false, error: 'no such host call' });
+    } catch (e) { try { send(res, 500, { ok: false, error: String(e && e.message || e) }); } catch {} }
+  });
+  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { control.once('error', reject); control.listen(Number(controlPort) || 0, '127.0.0.1', resolve); });
+  const port = control.address().port;
+  if (registry) {
+    try { mkdirSync(path.dirname(registry), { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(registry, JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, port, build, startedAt: new Date().toISOString() })); } catch (e) { log('host.json not written: ' + (e && e.message)); }
+    process.on('exit', () => { try { unlinkSync(registry); } catch {} });
+  }
+  log(`listening on 127.0.0.1:${port} (control), pid ${process.pid}`);
+  idleCheck();
+  return { port, servers, quit, startWindow, close: () => new Promise((r) => control.close(() => r())) };
+}

=== add src/llm/scheduler.mjs
new a4bed9016ffa45ba137ca4fb
+// scheduler.mjs - ONE QUEUE FOR EVERY MODEL CALL IN THE PROCESS (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md section 3; it replaces
+// token-budget.mjs once the call sites state their level).
+//
+// Every call from every window asks before it goes - `await scheduler.admit({ level, model, tokens, who, server,
+// signal })`, awaited in the caller's own async context (the scheduler only resolves a promise; it never runs the
+// caller's work from its own timers, so AsyncLocalStorage contexts survive) - and says what it used when it ends
+// (`ticket.release(usage)`).
+//
+//   level 0  the main agent, side chat, compaction, anything the developer waits on: never waits, only counted
+//   level 1  the bookkeeper
+//   level 2  sub-agents (the review checker included)
+//   level 3  Heads up, the dev check, the skills maintainer, recall, commit suggestions - the rest
+//
+// Waiting calls go level 1 first, then 2, then 3; oldest first within a level, round-robin across windows (`server`).
+// A level-L call may go while the minute's use plus its tokens leaves the share kept for the levels before it:
+//   reserve(L) = limit x 0.4 x (L / 3) x max(0, 1 - waited / patience(L))
+// - the reserve melts as it waits; past maxWait(L) it goes whatever the count. Requests a minute are checked the same
+// way. At most `backgroundInflight` calls of level >= 1 are in flight at once (process-wide).
+//
+// The edge fixes token-budget.mjs earned are kept: the abort listener is taken off when a wait ends (one long-lived
+// signal across hundreds of waits left hundreds of listeners); a call bigger than the whole minute goes as soon as the
+// minute is empty; a stopped call stops waiting; a 429 teaches a model with no limit 0.9 x the last minute.
+//
+// No polling: one timer for the whole queue, set to the next moment anything can change for a waiter (a call leaving
+// the window, a reserve melting enough, a 429 hold or patience ending, a maxWait). Releases, aborts and limit changes
+// look again at once. The timer is cancelled when nobody waits.
+
+export const SCH_WINDOW_MS = 60000;
+export const SCH_RESERVE_SHARE = 0.4;
+// by level; CB_TB_PATIENCE_MS / CB_TB_MAX_WAIT_MS (the old budget's names, kept: tests shorten the waits with them)
+// set level 2's, and the others scale with it (level 1 half of it, level 3 one and a half)
+const SCH_PAT2 = Number(process.env.CB_TB_PATIENCE_MS) || 60000, SCH_MAX2 = Number(process.env.CB_TB_MAX_WAIT_MS) || 120000;
+export const SCH_PATIENCE_MS = [0, SCH_PAT2 / 2, SCH_PAT2, SCH_PAT2 * 1.5];
+export const SCH_MAX_WAIT_MS = [0, SCH_MAX2 / 2, SCH_MAX2, SCH_MAX2 * 1.5];
+export const SCH_BACKGROUND_INFLIGHT = Number(process.env.CB_LLM_BACKGROUND_INFLIGHT) || 6;
+
+const schK = (n) => (n >= 10000 ? `${Math.round(n / 1000)}K` : String(Math.round(n)));
+const schPos = (v) => { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : 0; };
+/** A level as the scheduler takes it: 1..3 wait, anything else (missing, 0, junk) is 0 - never held. */
+export function schLevel(level) { const n = Math.floor(Number(level)); return n >= 3 ? 3 : n >= 1 ? n : 0; }
+/** The share of `limit` a level-`level` call leaves for the levels before it, having waited `waited` ms. */
+export function schReserve(limit, level, waited = 0) {
+  const L = schLevel(level);
+  if (!L) return 0;
+  return limit * SCH_RESERVE_SHARE * (L / 3) * Math.max(0, 1 - waited / SCH_PATIENCE_MS[L]);
+}
+const schAbortError = (who) => Object.assign(new Error(`${who} was stopped while it waited for its turn`), { name: 'AbortError' });
+// (the listener is taken off when the pause ends - token-budget's edge sweep, 2026-11-03)
+function schDefaultSleep(ms, signal) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    if (signal && signal.aborted) { resolve(); return; }
+    const stop = () => { clearTimeout(t); resolve(); };
+    const t = setTimeout(() => { if (signal) signal.removeEventListener('abort', stop); resolve(); }, ms);
+    if (signal) signal.addEventListener('abort', stop, { once: true });
+  });
+}
+
+/**
+ * `limitsFor(model)` -> { tokensPerMinute, requestsPerMinute } (0 / missing = none; a bare number = tokens a minute),
+ * read at every look so Settings apply live; `setLimitsFor(fn)` swaps it and looks again. `sleep(ms, signal)` must
+ * resolve when the signal aborts (the scheduler cancels its timer that way).
+ */
+export function createScheduler({ limitsFor = () => ({}), now = () => Date.now(), sleep = schDefaultSleep, log = () => {}, backgroundInflight = SCH_BACKGROUND_INFLIGHT } = {}) {
+  let limitsFn = limitsFor;
+  const cap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Number(backgroundInflight)) || SCH_BACKGROUND_INFLIGHT);
+  const models = new Map();   // model -> state
+  const queue = [];           // waiters, in arrival order
+  const served = [null, new Map(), new Map(), new Map()];   // level -> server -> serial of its last admission
+  let seq = 0, serial = 0, bgInflight = 0, timer = null;
+
+  const stateOf = (model) => {
+    const k = String(model || '');
+    let st = models.get(k);
+    if (!st) { st = { key: k, entries: [], learnedTpm: 0, learnedRpm: 0, throttledUntil: 0, inflight: 0, inflightBg: 0, gateway: null }; models.set(k, st); }
+    return st;
+  };
+  // the window: entries { at, tokens, req } sorted by at; one leaves exactly SCH_WINDOW_MS after it went
+  const windowOf = (st) => {
+    const cut = now() - SCH_WINDOW_MS, es = st.entries;
+    let i = 0;
+    while (i < es.length && es[i].at <= cut) i++;
+    if (i) es.splice(0, i);
+    return es;
+  };
+  const insert = (st, e) => {
+    const es = st.entries;
+    let i = es.length;
+    while (i > 0 && es[i - 1].at > e.at) i--;
+    es.splice(i, 0, e);
+    return e;
+  };
+  const usedOf = (st) => windowOf(st).reduce((a, e) => a + e.tokens, 0);
+  const requestsOf = (st) => windowOf(st).reduce((a, e) => a + e.req, 0);
+  const configured = (st) => {
+    let raw;
+    try { raw = limitsFn(st.key); } catch { raw = null; }
+    if (typeof raw === 'number' || typeof raw === 'string') return { tokensPerMinute: schPos(raw), requestsPerMinute: 0 };
+    return { tokensPerMinute: schPos(raw && raw.tokensPerMinute), requestsPerMinute: schPos(raw && raw.requestsPerMinute) };
+  };
+  const limitsOfState = (st) => {
+    const c = configured(st);
+    return { tokensPerMinute: c.tokensPerMinute || st.learnedTpm || 0, requestsPerMinute: c.requestsPerMinute || st.learnedRpm || 0 };
+  };
+
+  // ── may this waiter go now? { go } or { go: false, why, wakeAt } ──
+  // A count of `need` against `limit` with `used` in the window: it fits while used + need <= limit - reserve(L,
+  // waited). Not fitting, the next look is when the oldest entry leaves the window or the reserve has melted enough.
+  // Returns null when it fits, else when to look again.
+  const fitCheck = (limit, used, need, w, t, oldestOut) => {
+    const L = w.level, P = SCH_PATIENCE_MS[L], waited = t - w.t0;
+    // (bigger than the whole minute: no wait makes it fit - it goes as soon as the minute is empty; live, 2026-11-03,
+    // Heads up's 160K record against a 150K limit)
+    if (need >= limit) return used > 0 ? oldestOut : null;
+    const room = limit - used - need, r0 = limit * SCH_RESERVE_SHARE * (L / 3);
+    if (room + 1e-9 >= schReserve(limit, L, waited)) return null;
+    const melted = room >= 0 && r0 > 0 ? w.t0 + Math.ceil(P * (1 - room / r0)) : Infinity;
+    return Math.min(oldestOut, melted);
+  };
+  const check = (w, t) => {
+    if (bgInflight >= cap) return { go: false, why: 'inflight', wakeAt: Infinity };
+    const L = w.level, waited = t - w.t0;
+    const late = w.t0 + SCH_MAX_WAIT_MS[L];
+    if (waited >= SCH_MAX_WAIT_MS[L]) return { go: true };
+    const st = w.st, P = SCH_PATIENCE_MS[L];
+    // (the endpoint said too many: hold until its wait is over, up to the patience - then the count decides)
+    if (st.throttledUntil > t && waited < P) return { go: false, why: 'throttled', wakeAt: Math.min(late, st.throttledUntil, w.t0 + P) };
+    const lim = limitsOfState(st);
+    if (!lim.tokensPerMinute && !lim.requestsPerMinute) return { go: true };
+    const es = windowOf(st);
+    const oldestOut = es.length ? es[0].at + SCH_WINDOW_MS : Infinity;
+    const byTokens = lim.tokensPerMinute ? fitCheck(lim.tokensPerMinute, usedOf(st), w.tokens, w, t, oldestOut) : null;
+    const byRequests = lim.requestsPerMinute ? fitCheck(lim.requestsPerMinute, requestsOf(st), 1, w, t, oldestOut) : null;
+    if (byTokens == null && byRequests == null) return { go: true };
+    return { go: false, why: 'count', wakeAt: Math.min(late, byTokens ?? Infinity, byRequests ?? Infinity) };
+  };
+
+  // level 1 first, then 2, then 3; within a level the window served longest ago first, and its waiters taken one at a
+  // time in turn with the other windows' (round-robin), each window's oldest first
+  const ordered = () => {
+    const out = [];
+    for (const L of [1, 2, 3]) {
+      const groups = new Map();
+      for (const w of queue) if (w.level === L) { if (!groups.has(w.server)) groups.set(w.server, []); groups.get(w.server).push(w); }
+      if (!groups.size) continue;
+      const last = served[L];
+      const servers = [...groups.keys()].sort((a, b) => (last.get(a) || 0) - (last.get(b) || 0) || groups.get(a)[0].seq - groups.get(b)[0].seq);
+      for (let i = 0; ; i++) {
+        let any = false;
+        for (const s of servers) { const g = groups.get(s); if (i < g.length) { out.push(g[i]); any = true; } }
+        if (!any) break;
+      }
+    }
+    return out;
+  };
+
+  const makeTicket = (st, level, tokens, t, waited) => {
+    const entry = insert(st, { at: t, tokens, req: 1, level });
+    const bg = level >= 1;
+    st.inflight++;
+    if (bg) { bgInflight++; st.inflightBg++; }
+    let released = false;
+    return {
+      level, model: st.key, waited,
+      /** The call ended: its real usage (or null to keep the estimate), counted at the time it went; frees its slot. */
+      release(usage) {
+        if (released) return;
+        released = true;
+        if (usage && typeof usage === 'object') {
+          const p = Number(usage.promptTokens ?? usage.prompt_tokens), c = Number(usage.completionTokens ?? usage.completion_tokens);
+          if (Number.isFinite(p) && p >= 0) entry.tokens = Math.round(p);
+          if (Number.isFinite(c) && c > 0) entry.tokens += Math.round(c);
+        }
+        st.inflight--;
+        if (bg) { bgInflight--; st.inflightBg--; }
+        pump();
+      },
+    };
+  };
+
+  const unlisten = (w) => { if (w.signal && w.onAbort) w.signal.removeEventListener('abort', w.onAbort); w.onAbort = null; };
+  const go = (w, t) => {
+    queue.splice(queue.indexOf(w), 1);
+    unlisten(w);
+    served[w.level].set(w.server, ++serial);
+    const waited = t - w.t0;
+    if (w.said) log(`scheduler: ${w.who} went after ${Math.round(waited / 1000)}s (level ${w.level})`);
+    w.resolve(makeTicket(w.st, w.level, w.tokens, t, waited));
+  };
+
+  const cancelTimer = () => { if (timer) { const tm = timer; timer = null; tm.ac.abort(); } };
+  const schedule = (at) => {
+    if (!queue.length || !Number.isFinite(at)) { cancelTimer(); return; }
+    const t = now(), due = Math.max(at, t + 1);
+    if (timer && timer.at <= due && timer.at > t) return;   // (one that fires sooner is there: it looks again then)
+    cancelTimer();
+    const me = { at: due, ac: new AbortController() };
+    timer = me;
+    const fire = () => { if (timer !== me) return; timer = null; pump(); };
+    Promise.resolve(sleep(due - t, me.ac.signal)).then(fire, fire);
+  };
+
+  function pump() {
+    const t = now();
+    const blocked = new Set();   // models whose earlier waiter could not go: the later ones on it keep their place
+    let wakeAt = Infinity;
+    for (const w of ordered()) {
+      if (bgInflight >= cap) break;   // (a release looks again)
+      if (blocked.has(w.st) && t - w.t0 < SCH_MAX_WAIT_MS[w.level]) { wakeAt = Math.min(wakeAt, w.t0 + SCH_MAX_WAIT_MS[w.level]); continue; }
+      const c = check(w, t);
+      if (c.go) { go(w, t); continue; }
+      wakeAt = Math.min(wakeAt, c.wakeAt);
+      if (c.why !== 'inflight') blocked.add(w.st);
+    }
+    schedule(wakeAt);
+  }
+
+  const describe = (st) => {
+    const lim = limitsOfState(st);
+    const parts = [];
+    if (lim.tokensPerMinute) parts.push(`${schK(usedOf(st))} of ${schK(lim.tokensPerMinute)} tokens`);
+    if (lim.requestsPerMinute) parts.push(`${requestsOf(st)} of ${lim.requestsPerMinute} requests`);
+    let s = parts.length ? `${parts.join(', ')} in the last minute` : 'no limit';
+    if (st.throttledUntil > now()) s += ', and the endpoint said too many';
+    if (bgInflight >= cap) s += `, ${bgInflight} background calls in flight`;
+    return s;
+  };
+
+  return {
+    /**
+     * Wait for this call's turn. Resolves with a ticket { waited, level, model, release(usage) } when it may go;
+     * rejects with an AbortError when `signal` aborts first. Level 0 resolves at once (counted, never held).
+     */
+    admit({ level = 0, model = '', tokens = 0, who = 'a call', server = '', signal = null } = {}) {
+      const L = schLevel(level), st = stateOf(model), n = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(tokens) || 0));
+      if (!L) return Promise.resolve(makeTicket(st, 0, n, now(), 0));
+      if (signal && signal.aborted) return Promise.reject(schAbortError(who));
+      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+        const w = { seq: ++seq, level: L, st, tokens: n, who: String(who), server: String(server ?? ''), t0: now(), resolve, reject, signal, onAbort: null, said: false };
+        if (signal) {
+          w.onAbort = () => {
+            const i = queue.indexOf(w);
+            if (i < 0) return;
+            queue.splice(i, 1);
+            w.onAbort = null;
+            if (w.said) log(`scheduler: ${w.who} stopped while it waited (level ${L})`);
+            reject(schAbortError(w.who));
+            pump();   // (it may have held the ones behind it)
+          };
+          signal.addEventListener('abort', w.onAbort, { once: true });
+        }
+        queue.push(w);
+        pump();
+        if (queue.includes(w)) { w.said = true; log(`scheduler: ${w.who} waits (level ${L}) - ${describe(st)}, the agent first`); }
+      });
+    },
+    /** A call made outside admit (a hedge's second copy, a retry): counted, not held. */
+    note(model, tokens, level = 0) {
+      insert(stateOf(model), { at: now(), tokens: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(tokens) || 0)), req: 1, level: schLevel(level) });
+    },
+    /** A 429 for `model`: levels 1-3 on it hold `ms` (bounded by their patience); a model with no limit learns one. */
+    throttled(model, ms) {
+      const st = stateOf(model);
+      const t = now();
+      st.throttledUntil = Math.max(st.throttledUntil, t + Math.max(1000, Number(ms) || 0));
+      const c = configured(st);
+      if (!c.tokensPerMinute) {
+        const u = usedOf(st);
+        if (u > 0) { const lim = Math.max(1, Math.round(u * 0.9)); if (!st.learnedTpm || lim < st.learnedTpm) { st.learnedTpm = lim; log(`scheduler: ${st.key} said too many at ${schK(u)} tokens in a minute - paced at ${schK(lim)} a minute from now`); } }
+      }
+      if (!c.requestsPerMinute) {
+        const r = requestsOf(st);
+        if (r > 0) { const lim = Math.max(1, Math.floor(r * 0.9)); if (!st.learnedRpm || lim < st.learnedRpm) { st.learnedRpm = lim; log(`scheduler: ${st.key} said too many at ${r} requests in a minute - paced at ${lim} a minute from now`); } }
+      }
+      pump();
+    },
+    /**
+     * The gateway's tokens-a-minute figures for `model` ({ limit, remaining, at }, or parseBudgetHeaders' result):
+     * when it has seen more spent than the count has (other users of the key), the count is raised to match - an entry
+     * at `at` that leaves the window like any other.
+     */
+    reading(model, figures) {
+      let f = figures;
+      if (f && f.tokens && typeof f.tokens === 'object') f = { ...f.tokens, at: f.at };
+      if (!f) return;
+      const limit = Number(f.limit), remaining = Number(f.remaining);
+      if (!Number.isFinite(limit) || !Number.isFinite(remaining) || f.limit == null || f.remaining == null) return;
+      const st = stateOf(model), t = now();
+      const at = Number.isFinite(Number(f.at)) && f.at != null ? Math.min(Number(f.at), t) : t;
+      st.gateway = { limit, remaining, at };
+      if (at <= t - SCH_WINDOW_MS) return;
+      const theirs = limit - remaining, ours = usedOf(st);
+      if (theirs > ours) insert(st, { at, tokens: Math.round(theirs - ours), req: 0, level: -1, external: true });
+    },
+    /** Swap the limits (Settings changed) - or, with no argument, look again at the same function's answers. */
+    setLimitsFor(fn) { if (typeof fn === 'function') limitsFn = fn; pump(); },
+    used(model) { return usedOf(stateOf(model)); },
+    requests(model) { return requestsOf(stateOf(model)); },
+    limitsOf(model) { return limitsOfState(stateOf(model)); },
+    /** For a status view: per model what the minute holds, its limits, who waits and who is in flight. */
+    snapshot() {
+      const t = now();
+      const out = { at: t, background: { inflight: bgInflight, cap }, waiting: queue.length, models: {} };
+      for (const st of models.values()) {
+        const es = windowOf(st);
+        const waits = queue.filter((w) => w.st === st);
+        if (!es.length && !waits.length && !st.inflight && !st.learnedTpm && !st.learnedRpm && !(st.throttledUntil > t) && !st.gateway) continue;
+        const waiting = {};
+        for (const L of [1, 2, 3]) {
+          const ws = waits.filter((w) => w.level === L);
+          waiting[L] = { count: ws.length, oldestMs: ws.length ? t - Math.min(...ws.map((w) => w.t0)) : 0 };
+        }
+        out.models[st.key] = {
+          used: es.reduce((a, e) => a + e.tokens, 0),
+          requests: es.reduce((a, e) => a + e.req, 0),
+          external: es.reduce((a, e) => a + (e.external ? e.tokens : 0), 0),
+          limits: limitsOfState(st),
+          configured: configured(st),
+          learned: { tokensPerMinute: st.learnedTpm, requestsPerMinute: st.learnedRpm },
+          throttledUntil: st.throttledUntil > t ? st.throttledUntil : 0,
+          gateway: st.gateway,
+          waiting,
+          inflight: { all: st.inflight, background: st.inflightBg },
+        };
+      }
+      return out;
+    },
+    /** Stop: every waiter is rejected (AbortError) and the timer cancelled. */
+    close() {
+      cancelTimer();
+      for (const w of queue.splice(0)) { unlisten(w); w.reject(schAbortError(w.who)); }
+    },
+  };
+}
+

+// LABELS WITH LINE BREAKS AND A LITTLE MARKUP (the developer, 2026-11-18: "in diagrams the agent puts "<br/>" for a
+// line break but we're not rendering"): <br>, <br/>, <br /> split a label into lines; <b>/<strong>, <i>/<em> and <u>
+// style the words between them; any other tag is taken out and its words kept. Nothing from the label is put into the
+// page as markup: every piece is escaped text inside an SVG tspan.
+const _MM_BR = /<br\s*\/?>/gi;
+const _MM_TAG = /<\s*(\/?)\s*([a-z][a-z0-9]*)\b[^>]*>/gi;
+function _mmLines(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).split(_MM_BR); }
+// a line's words, as they read (tags out, the few entities a label uses turned back)
+function _mmPlain(line) {
+  return String(line).replace(_MM_TAG, '').replace(/&nbsp;/gi, ' ').replace(/&lt;/gi, '<').replace(/&gt;/gi, '>').replace(/&quot;/gi, '"').replace(/&#39;/g, "'").replace(/&amp;/gi, '&');
+}
+// a label as one line of words (a title, a sequence message)
+function mmPlainLabel(s) { return _mmLines(s).map(_mmPlain).join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
+// one line as tspans: the styled runs between the tags it knows
+function _mmLineSpans(line) {
+  const st = { b: 0, i: 0, u: 0 }, out = [];
+  let last = 0, m;
+  const push = (txt) => {
+    const t = _mmPlain(txt); if (!t) return;
+    const a = (st.b ? ' font-weight="700"' : '') + (st.i ? ' font-style="italic"' : '') + (st.u ? ' text-decoration="underline"' : '');
+    out.push(a ? `<tspan${a}>${mmEsc(t)}</tspan>` : mmEsc(t));
+  };
+  // (a regex of its own: _mmPlain's replace with the shared one sets its lastIndex back to 0 - an endless loop)
+  const re = new RegExp(_MM_TAG.source, 'gi');
+  while ((m = re.exec(line))) {
+    push(line.slice(last, m.index)); last = m.index + m[0].length;
+    const k = { b: 'b', strong: 'b', i: 'i', em: 'i', u: 'u' }[m[2].toLowerCase()];
+    if (k) st[k] = Math.max(0, st[k] + (m[1] ? -1 : 1));
+  }
+  push(line.slice(last));
+  return out.join('');
+}
+const MM_LINE_H = 15;
+// the <text> of a label centred on (x, cy): one tspan a line, the lines stacked about cy
+function _mmText(x, cy, label, attrs, lineH = MM_LINE_H) {
+  const lines = _mmLines(label), n = lines.length;
