=== add src/plugins/filter-worker.mjs
new 6258e7f64cd6f3114068a850
+/**
+ * filter-worker.mjs - EVERY TEXT A WORKER FILTER MASKS IS MASKED OFF THE SERVER'S THREAD (the developer, 2026-11-15: "I
+ * think it would be a good idea to put things on a worker that could hang the process as a safety net"; and since one
+ * process hosts a server per window, ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md section 5: a pool for the process, every text masked there,
+ * unmasking too). Prod, 2026-11-15: piishape masked the bookkeeper's whole record - a few megabytes - on the server's
+ * one thread, a pattern in it took minutes, nothing else ran (not even /api/health), and after 3 minutes the container
+ * manager restarted code_boss mid-run. With six windows in one process, ten seconds a megabyte on the main thread was
+ * ten seconds of every window.
+ *
+ * A filter whose plugin marks it `worker: true` masks (and unmasks) on a thread of the process's pool: a copy of the
+ * plugin is loaded there from its file - one copy per plugin per window server, each on the thread it was first given
+ * (what it keeps in memory stays with it) - and its `host` is a stand-in whose calls - tokens.issue, vault.read, log,
+ * ... - are answered by the server's thread, synchronously as the plugin expects (Atomics.wait in the worker, the answer
+ * read with receiveMessageOnPort), and in that window's own scope. So there is one store, one secret, one list of issued
+ * tokens. Only host.request is not given there: a question to the developer is never asked twice.
+ *
+ * THE PIECES OF ONE CALL GO TOGETHER: run({ texts }) masks a list of texts in one round trip (a mask() split by turn
+ * markers, a maskDeep's strings - a postMessage per one-kilobyte piece cost more than masking it).
+ *
+ * WHILE IT MASKS, THE SERVER RUNS ON: requests, the page, /api/health. AND IT HAS A LIMIT IT CAN KEEP: past it the
+ * thread is ended and the text is refused, as a filter that fails always refuses (fail-closed). A thread runs one job
+ * at a time; the jobs waiting for it go to the new thread that replaces it.
+ *
+ * What the plugin keeps only in memory goes with each job (filter.workerState() on the server's copy,
+ * filter.setWorkerState(s) on the worker's); filter.afterWorkerMask() runs on the worker's copy after each, before the
+ * answer (piishape writes the values it learned to the store there); filter.workerReport() there and
+ * filter.takeWorkerReport(r) here bring back what the server's copy shows (piishape: what was masked, and where an id
+ * was seen).
+ */
+import { Worker, MessageChannel } from 'node:worker_threads';
+import { availableParallelism } from 'node:os';
+import { hsCurrent, hsRun } from '../host-scope.mjs';
+
+// (CB_FILTER_WORKER_MIN: a text shorter than this stays on the server's thread - 0, the default, sends every one)
+const fwEnvMin = process.env.CB_FILTER_WORKER_MIN;
+/** A text this long or longer is masked on a worker: every text by default (CB_FILTER_WORKER_MIN overrides). */
+export const FILTER_WORKER_MIN = fwEnvMin != null && fwEnvMin !== '' && Number.isFinite(Number(fwEnvMin)) ? Math.max(0, Number(fwEnvMin)) : 0;
+
+/** How long a worker may mask a text: a minute and a half, and a minute more for each megabyte, at most 10 minutes. */
+export const filterWorkerLimitMs = (chars, floorMs = 0) => Number(process.env.CB_FILTER_WORKER_LIMIT_MS) || Math.max(floorMs, Math.min(10 * 60 * 1000, 90000 + Math.ceil((chars || 0) / 1048576) * 60000));   // (CB_FILTER_WORKER_LIMIT_MS: a test's)
+
+/** The pool's size: min(4, cores - 1), at least one (CB_FILTER_WORKER_THREADS overrides). */
+export const FILTER_WORKER_THREADS = Math.max(1, Number(process.env.CB_FILTER_WORKER_THREADS) || Math.min(4, (() => { try { return availableParallelism(); } catch { return 2; } })() - 1));
+
+// (the worker: plain CommonJS, run from this text - it has nothing of the bundle; indented, so the build's check of the
+// bundle's one flat scope does not take its names for the bundle's)
+const FW_CODE = String.raw`
+  const { parentPort, receiveMessageOnPort, workerData } = require('node:worker_threads');
+  const { pathToFileURL } = require('node:url');
+  const { sab, port } = workerData;
+  const flag = new Int32Array(sab);
+  function hostCall(ik, p, args) {
+    Atomics.store(flag, 0, 0);
+    parentPort.postMessage({ type: 'host', ik, path: p, args });
+    Atomics.wait(flag, 0, 0, 120000);
+    const m = receiveMessageOnPort(port);
+    if (!m) throw new Error('code_boss did not answer the masking thread (' + p + ')');
+    if (m.message.error != null) throw new Error(m.message.error);
+    return m.message.async ? Promise.resolve(m.message.value) : m.message.value;
+  }
+  function makeHost(ik, shape) {
+    const host = shape.data || {};
+    for (const p of shape.fns || []) {
+      const parts = p.split('.');
+      let o = host;
+      for (const k of parts.slice(0, -1)) { if (!o[k] || typeof o[k] !== 'object') o[k] = {}; o = o[k]; }
+      o[parts[parts.length - 1]] = p === 'request' ? () => Promise.reject(new Error('host.request is not available on the masking thread'))
+        : (...args) => hostCall(ik, p, args);
+    }
+    return host;
+  }
+  // one copy of a plugin per window server (ik): a module of its own, as each window's plugin manager has
+  const copies = new Map();
+  function copyFor(msg) {
+    let c = copies.get(msg.ik);
+    if (c) return c;
+    if (!msg.file || !msg.shape) return Promise.reject(new Error('the plugin was not loaded on this thread'));
+    c = (async () => {
+      const m = await import(pathToFileURL(msg.file).href + '?filter-worker=' + encodeURIComponent(msg.ik) + '-' + Date.now());
+      const plugin = m.default || m;
+      if (plugin && typeof plugin.init === 'function') await plugin.init(makeHost(msg.ik, msg.shape));
+      return plugin;
+    })();
+    c.catch(() => {});
+    copies.set(msg.ik, c);
+    return c;
+  }
+  const errText = (e) => String((e && e.message) || e);
+  parentPort.on('message', async (msg) => {
+    if (!msg) return;
+    if (msg.type === 'drop') { copies.delete(msg.ik); return; }
+    if (msg.type !== 'job') return;
+    let f = null;
+    // (a plugin that cannot be loaded here, or whose filter is not where it is on the server's thread: said as such -
+    // the text is masked on the server's thread instead, as before there were workers)
+    try {
+      const plugin = await copyFor(msg);
+      f = ((plugin && plugin.filters) || [])[msg.index];
+      if (!f || f.name !== msg.name) throw new Error('the filter "' + msg.name + '" is not in its plugin on the masking thread');
+      if (msg.op === 'unmask' && typeof f.unmask !== 'function') throw new Error('the filter "' + msg.name + '" has no unmask');
+    } catch (e) { copies.delete(msg.ik); parentPort.postMessage({ type: 'done', id: msg.id, error: errText(e), loadFailed: true }); return; }
+    let piece = -1;
+    try {
+      if (msg.state != null && typeof f.setWorkerState === 'function') f.setWorkerState(msg.state);
+      const outs = [];
+      for (piece = 0; piece < msg.texts.length; piece++) {
+        // (this thread's own event loop gets a turn between pieces: without it a job of 256 one-kilobyte pieces took
+        // 9.7 s here against 1.2 s with it - measured, 2026-11-18; the plugin's timers and the engine's housekeeping
+        // wait for that turn)
+        if (piece) await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r));
+        outs.push(msg.op === 'unmask' ? await f.unmask(msg.texts[piece], msg.ctx) : await f.mask(msg.texts[piece], msg.ctx));
+      }
+      piece = -1;
+      if (typeof f.afterWorkerMask === 'function') { try { await f.afterWorkerMask(); } catch (e) { /* the mask stands */ } }
+      let report;
+      if (typeof f.workerReport === 'function') { try { report = f.workerReport(); } catch (e) { report = undefined; } }
+      try { parentPort.postMessage({ type: 'done', id: msg.id, outs, report }); }
+      catch (e) { parentPort.postMessage({ type: 'done', id: msg.id, error: 'its answer could not be sent back: ' + errText(e) }); }
+    } catch (e) {
+      // (what it learned before the failure is still written - the tokens it issued are out there)
+      if (typeof f.afterWorkerMask === 'function') { try { await f.afterWorkerMask(); } catch (e2) { /* the refusal stands */ } }
+      parentPort.postMessage({ type: 'done', id: msg.id, error: errText(e), refusal: !!(e && e.refusal), piece });
+    }
+  });
+`;
+
+// a value as it can cross to the other thread: as it is, or as JSON, or nothing (a function, a socket)
+function fwCloneSafe(v) {
+  if (v === undefined) return undefined;
+  try { return structuredClone(v); } catch { /* not as it is */ }
+  try { return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v)); } catch { return undefined; }
+}
+/** The host as the worker can have it: its plain values, and the paths of its functions (answered from here). */
+export function fwHostShape(host) {
+  const data = {}, fns = [], seen = new Set();
+  const walk = (o, into, pre, depth) => {
+    if (!o || typeof o !== 'object' || seen.has(o) || depth > 3) return;
+    seen.add(o);
+    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o)) {
+      const p = pre ? pre + '.' + k : k;
+      if (typeof v === 'function') fns.push(p);
+      else if (v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) && Object.getPrototypeOf(v) === Object.prototype && k !== 'state') { into[k] = {}; walk(v, into[k], p, depth + 1); }
+      else { const c = fwCloneSafe(v); if (c !== undefined) into[k] = c; }
+    }
+  };
+  walk(host, data, '', 0);
+  return { data, fns };
+}
+// a mask call's ctx, as it can cross: its plain values (a function in it - a callback - stays here)
+function fwCtxShape(ctx) {
+  const out = {};
+  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(ctx || {})) { if (typeof v === 'function') continue; const c = fwCloneSafe(v); if (c !== undefined) out[k] = c; }
+  return out;
+}
+// the function at `p` in the host, called as a method of what holds it
+function fwCallAt(host, p, args) {
+  const parts = String(p).split('.');
+  let o = host;
+  for (const k of parts.slice(0, -1)) { o = o && o[k]; if (!o) throw new Error('the host has no ' + p); }
+  const fn = o && o[parts[parts.length - 1]];
+  if (typeof fn !== 'function') throw new Error('the host has no ' + p);
+  return fn.apply(o, Array.isArray(args) ? args : []);
+}
+
+// ── THE POOL: one for the process ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// threads[i]: { i, worker, flag, port, loaded: Set(ik), hi: [], lo: [], running: job|null, dead }
+// copies: ik -> { ik, ti, file, host, client, pluginName }   (a copy of a plugin for one window server's host)
+// homes: pluginName + host -> the thread its copies live on (a new version of the plugin stays there)
+const fwPool = { threads: [], copies: new Map(), homes: new Map(), hostIds: new WeakMap(), hostSeq: 0, jobSeq: 0 };
+const fwHostId = (host) => { let id = fwPool.hostIds.get(host); if (!id) { id = 'h' + (++fwPool.hostSeq); fwPool.hostIds.set(host, id); } return id; };
+
+function fwThreadEnd(t, why) {
+  if (t.dead) return;
+  t.dead = true;
+  try { t.worker.terminate(); } catch { /* gone */ }
+  const job = t.running; t.running = null;
+  if (job) { clearTimeout(job.timer); job.rej(new Error(why)); }
+  // (the jobs still waiting: on the thread that replaces it - none of them ran here)
+  if (t.hi.length || t.lo.length) setImmediate(() => fwPump(t.i));
+}
+function fwThreadAt(i) {
+  let t = fwPool.threads[i];
+  if (t && !t.dead) return t;
+  const hi = t ? t.hi : [], lo = t ? t.lo : [];
+  const sab = new SharedArrayBuffer(4), flag = new Int32Array(sab);
+  const { port1, port2 } = new MessageChannel();
+  // (made outside any window's scope: the thread is the process's, not the window's that first needed it)
+  const worker = hsRun(null, () => new Worker(FW_CODE, { eval: true, workerData: { sab, port: port2 }, transferList: [port2] }));
+  try { port1.unref(); } catch { /* (only posted to: it must not keep the process alive) */ }
+  t = { i, worker, flag, port: port1, loaded: new Set(), hi, lo, running: null, dead: false };
+  fwPool.threads[i] = t;
+  const me = t;
+  worker.on('message', async (m) => {
+    if (!m) return;
+    if (m.type === 'host') {
+      let reply;
+      const c = fwPool.copies.get(m.ik);
+      try {
+        if (!c) throw new Error('that plugin copy was let go (its window closed)');
+        // (answered in its own window's scope: a log line, a setting, a store read are that window's)
+        let v = hsRun(c.client.srv, () => fwCallAt(c.host, m.path, m.args));
+        const isP = !!(v && typeof v.then === 'function');
+        if (isP) v = await v;
+        reply = { value: fwCloneSafe(v), async: isP };
+      } catch (err) { reply = { error: String((err && err.message) || err) }; }
+      try { me.port.postMessage(reply); } catch (err) { try { me.port.postMessage({ error: 'its answer could not be sent: ' + String((err && err.message) || err) }); } catch { /* ended */ } }
+      Atomics.store(me.flag, 0, 1); Atomics.notify(me.flag, 0);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (m.type === 'done') {
+      const job = me.running;
+      if (!job || job.id !== m.id) return;
+      me.running = null;
+      clearTimeout(job.timer);
+      if (m.error != null) {
+        if (m.loadFailed) me.loaded.delete(job.copy.ik);
+        job.rej(Object.assign(new Error(m.error), m.refusal ? { refusal: true } : {}, m.loadFailed ? { loadFailed: true } : {}, Number.isInteger(m.piece) && m.piece >= 0 ? { piece: m.piece } : {}));
+      } else job.res({ outs: m.outs, report: m.report });
+      fwPump(me.i);
+    }
+  });
+  worker.on('error', (err) => { const job = me.running; if (job) { job.client.stats.failed++; job.client.log(`filter worker for ${job.copy.pluginName} failed: ${(err && err.message) || err}`); } fwThreadEnd(me, 'the masking thread failed: ' + ((err && err.message) || err)); });
+  worker.on('exit', () => fwThreadEnd(me, 'the masking thread ended'));
+  // (after the listeners: a 'message' listener added after unref() holds the process open again - measured)
+  worker.unref();
+  return t;
+}
+function fwPump(i) {
+  let t = fwPool.threads[i];
+  if (t && !t.dead && t.running) return;
+  if (!t || (!t.hi.length && !t.lo.length)) return;
+  const job = t.hi.length ? t.hi.shift() : t.lo.shift();
+  if (job.cancelled) { fwPump(i); return; }
+  t = fwThreadAt(i);
+  const c = job.copy;
+  if (!fwPool.copies.has(c.ik)) { job.rej(new Error('the masking thread was let go (its window closed)')); fwPump(i); return; }
+  const first = !t.loaded.has(c.ik);
+  t.running = job;
+  job.t0 = Date.now();
+  job.timer = setTimeout(() => {
+    job.client.stats.ended++;
+    job.client.log(`filter worker for ${c.pluginName}: ${job.filterName} went past its ${Math.round(job.limitMs / 1000)} s on ${job.chars} characters - the thread was ended`);
+    fwThreadEnd(t, `it went past its ${Math.round(job.limitMs / 1000)} s on a text of ${job.chars} characters, on a thread of its own - the thread was ended, and code_boss went on`);
+  }, job.limitMs);
+  try {
+    t.worker.postMessage({ type: 'job', id: job.id, ik: c.ik, op: job.op, index: job.index, name: job.filterName, texts: job.texts, ctx: job.ctx, state: job.state, ...(first ? { file: c.file, shape: fwHostShape(c.host) } : {}) });
+    t.loaded.add(c.ik);
+  } catch (err) { clearTimeout(job.timer); t.running = null; job.rej(err); fwPump(i); }
+}
+// the thread a plugin's copies for one host live on: the one with the fewest copies, then the lowest number
+function fwHomeFor(key) {
+  let ti = fwPool.homes.get(key);
+  if (ti != null) return ti;
+  const n = new Array(FILTER_WORKER_THREADS).fill(0);
+  for (const c of fwPool.copies.values()) if (c.ti < n.length) n[c.ti]++;
+  ti = n.indexOf(Math.min(...n));
+  fwPool.homes.set(key, ti);
+  return ti;
+}
+function fwDrop(c) {
+  if (fwPool.copies.get(c.ik) !== c) return;
+  fwPool.copies.delete(c.ik);
+  const t = fwPool.threads[c.ti];
+  if (t && !t.dead && t.loaded.has(c.ik)) { t.loaded.delete(c.ik); try { t.worker.postMessage({ type: 'drop', ik: c.ik }); } catch { /* ended */ } }
+  // a thread no window has a copy on any more is ended (a closed window's modules go with it); the next job starts one
+  if (t && !t.dead && !t.running && !t.hi.length && !t.lo.length && ![...fwPool.copies.values()].some((x) => x.ti === c.ti)) fwThreadEnd(t, 'no plugin copies left on it');
+}
+/** What the process's pool is doing: { size, threads: [{ i, copies, waiting, busy }] } - for a report. */
+export function filterWorkerPoolReport() {
+  return { size: FILTER_WORKER_THREADS, threads: fwPool.threads.filter(Boolean).map((t) => ({ i: t.i, alive: !t.dead, copies: [...fwPool.copies.values()].filter((c) => c.ti === t.i).map((c) => c.pluginName), waiting: t.hi.length + t.lo.length, busy: !!t.running })) };
+}
+
+/**
+ * A server's door to the process's pool (each window's server makes one; the threads are shared). run() masks texts
+ * on its plugin's thread and answers what the filter answered; it throws when the filter threw, when the worker
+ * failed, and when it went past its limit (the worker is ended then). The jobs of one plugin for one server go one at
+ * a time, in order; an unmask goes before masks still waiting. stopAll() lets this server's copies go (done when its
+ * window's server closes).
+ */
+export function createFilterWorkers({ log = () => {}, disabled = /^(0|off|false|no)$/i.test(process.env.CB_FILTER_WORKER || '') } = {}) {
+  const stats = { runs: 0, ended: 0, failed: 0, lastMs: 0, lastChars: 0, maxMs: 0 };
+  const client = { srv: hsCurrent(), log: (m) => { try { log(m); } catch { /* the log is best effort */ } }, stats, copies: new Map(), jobs: new Set(), stopped: false };
+  function copyFor(pluginName, file, host) {
+    const hk = fwHostId(host), key = pluginName + '\u0000' + hk;
+    let c = client.copies.get(key);
+    if (c && c.file === file && fwPool.copies.has(c.ik)) return c;
+    const old = c;
+    const ti = fwHomeFor(key);
+    c = { ik: pluginName + '|' + hk + '|' + (++fwPool.jobSeq), ti, file, host, client, pluginName };
+    fwPool.copies.set(c.ik, c);
+    client.copies.set(key, c);
+    if (old) fwDrop(old);   // a new version of the plugin: a new copy, on the same thread (the old one let go after it is there)
+    return c;
+  }
+  /**
+   * Mask (op 'mask', the default) or unmask (op 'unmask') `texts` - or one `text` - with filter `index` (named
+   * `filterName`) of plugin `pluginName`, loaded from `file`. Answers a list for `texts`, a string for `text`.
+   * With `withReport`, answers { outs, report } (the plugin's workerReport).
+   */
+  function run({ pluginName, file, index, filterName, text, texts, op = 'mask', ctx, host, limitMs, state = null, withReport = false }) {
+    const many = Array.isArray(texts);
+    const list = many ? texts : [text];
+    if (client.stopped) return Promise.reject(new Error('code_boss is stopping'));
+    const c = copyFor(pluginName, file, host);
+    const chars = list.reduce((n, s) => n + (typeof s === 'string' ? s.length : 0), 0);
+    return new Promise((res, rej) => {
+      const job = { id: ++fwPool.jobSeq, copy: c, client, op, index, filterName, texts: list, ctx: fwCtxShape(ctx), state: fwCloneSafe(state), limitMs, chars, timer: null, t0: 0, cancelled: false };
+      job.res = (r) => {
+        client.jobs.delete(job);
+        stats.runs++; stats.lastMs = Date.now() - job.t0; stats.lastChars = chars; stats.maxMs = Math.max(stats.maxMs, stats.lastMs);
+        const outs = r && Array.isArray(r.outs) ? r.outs : [];
+        if (withReport) res({ outs: many ? outs : outs[0], report: r && r.report });
+        else res(many ? outs : outs[0]);
+      };
+      job.rej = (err) => { client.jobs.delete(job); rej(err); };
+      client.jobs.add(job);
+      const t = fwPool.threads[c.ti] || (fwPool.threads[c.ti] = { i: c.ti, worker: null, loaded: new Set(), hi: [], lo: [], running: null, dead: true });
+      (op === 'unmask' ? t.hi : t.lo).push(job);
+      fwPump(c.ti);
+    });
+  }
+  // (a plugin file that could not be loaded on a thread: masked on the server's thread from then on - said once)
+  const unloadable = new Set();
+  const cannotLoad = (f, why) => {
+    const k = String(f && f.pluginFile);
+    if (unloadable.has(k)) return;
+    unloadable.add(k); stats.failed++;
+    client.log(`${f && f.plugin}: it could not be loaded on a thread of its own (${String(why).slice(0, 200)}) - it masks on the server's thread, as before`);
+    for (const [key, c] of client.copies) if (c.file === k) { client.copies.delete(key); fwDrop(c); }
+  };
+  /** Whether a filter can be run here: on, a worker filter, its plugin's file and host known, and it loaded here before. */
+  const usable = (f) => !disabled && !client.stopped && !!(f && f.worker === true && f.pluginFile && f.pluginHost && Number.isInteger(f.filterIndex)) && !unloadable.has(String(f.pluginFile));
+  const report = () => ({ ...stats, threads: [...new Set([...client.copies.values()].filter((c) => fwPool.copies.has(c.ik)).map((c) => c.pluginName))], min: FILTER_WORKER_MIN, on: !disabled, pool: filterWorkerPoolReport() });
+  const stopAll = () => {
+    client.stopped = true;
+    for (const job of [...client.jobs]) {
+      const t = fwPool.threads[job.copy.ti];
+      if (t && t.running === job) fwThreadEnd(t, 'code_boss is stopping');   // (its work is stopped, as before: the thread goes; the others' wait for the next)
+      else { job.cancelled = true; job.rej(new Error('code_boss is stopping')); }
+    }
+    for (const [key, c] of client.copies) { fwDrop(c); fwPool.homes.delete(key); }
+    client.copies.clear();
+  };
+  // (a window's server that closes lets its copies go)
+  if (client.srv && Array.isArray(client.srv.onClose)) client.srv.onClose.push(stopAll);
+  return { run, usable, cannotLoad, report, stopAll };
+}

+const CB_LOG_SYNC = { on: false };

+function cbLogWrite(file, text, { after = null } = {}) {
+  if (!file) return;
+  if (CB_LOG_SYNC.on) {
+    cbLogFlushSync(file);

+    return;

+async function cbLogPump(file, q) {
+  q.busy = true;
+  try {
+    while (q.parts.length) {
+      const chunk = q.parts.splice(0).join(''), afters = q.after.splice(0);
+      let okw = true;
+      try {
+        if (!q.dirMade) { await mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true }); q.dirMade = true; }
+        await appendFile(file, chunk);
+      } catch (e) {
+        okw = false;
+        q.dirMade = false;   // (an external clean may have removed the folder: made again next time)
+        try { diagAppend(`${new Date().toISOString()}  WARN  [persist] build=${BUILD_VERSION_RUNTIME} pid=${process.pid} | ${path.basename(file)} append FAILED (${e && (e.code || e.message)}) - ${chunk.length} chars written here instead:\n${chunk.slice(0, 4000)}`); } catch {}
+      }
+      for (const f of afters) { try { await f(okw); } catch {} }
+    }
+  } finally { q.busy = false; if (!q.parts.length) _cbLogQ.delete(file); else cbLogPump(file, q); }
+}
+function cbLogFlushSync(only = null) {
+  for (const [file, q] of _cbLogQ) {
+    if (only && file !== only) continue;
+    if (!q.parts.length) continue;
+    const chunk = q.parts.splice(0).join('');
+    try { mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true }); appendFileSync(file, chunk); } catch {}
+  }

+  // -- A PLUGIN TELLS THE AGENT (the developer, 2026-11-19: a plugin with an HTTP server takes a webhook request, gives it
+  // to the agent, and the agent's answer goes back as the response; "the message to the agent ... shows up as a user
+  // message, I'd like it to look like a tool"). host.request('tell-agent', { text, label, reply = true, timeoutMs }):
+  // the message goes to THIS window's agent - into the running turn, or a fresh run - shown as a tool-shaped row naming
+  // the plugin. With reply (the default) it carries a request id and the agent answers with replyTo(id, "text") in a
+  // snippet (a run's last reply cannot be the answer: a message that came mid-run shares the run with other work); the
+  // call resolves with { id, replied: true, text } - the text unmasked, as a plugin's tool gets its arguments - or
+  // { id, replied: false, timedOut | aborted } (no answer within timeoutMs, 10 minutes by default; the plugin unloaded).
+  // reply: false resolves at once with { delivered: true, id }.
+  const _pluginReplies = new Map();   // request id -> { plugin, end, timer }
+  let _pluginReqN = 0;
+  function pluginTellAgent(pluginName, payload, signal) {
+    const p = payload && typeof payload === 'object' ? payload : { text: payload };
+    const text = String(p.text == null ? '' : p.text);
+    if (!text.trim()) throw new Error('tell-agent: payload.text (the message for the agent) is required');
+    if (!session.activeProject) throw new Error('tell-agent: no project is open in this window - there is no agent to tell');
+    const label = String(p.label || 'a message').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 120);
+    const wantReply = p.reply !== false;
+    const id = 'p' + (++_pluginReqN);
+    const head = `[${label} - from the plugin ${pluginName}${wantReply ? `, request ${id}` : ''}]`;
+    const tail = wantReply ? `\n\n[${pluginName} waits for your answer to this: give it with replyTo("${id}", "your answer") in a snippet - that text is what goes back, once. Answer it before other work if you can.]` : '';
+    const meta = { name: pluginName, label, id };
+    if (!wantReply) { wakeAgent(`${head}\n${text}`, { logId: `plugin ${pluginName} ${id}`, source: 'plugin', plugin: meta }); return Promise.resolve({ delivered: true, id }); }
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+      const ms = Number.isFinite(Number(p.timeoutMs)) && Number(p.timeoutMs) > 0 ? Number(p.timeoutMs) : 10 * 60 * 1000;
+      const onAbort = () => end({ replied: false, aborted: true });
+      const end = (r) => { const e = _pluginReplies.get(id); if (!e) return; _pluginReplies.delete(id); clearTimeout(e.timer); try { if (signal) signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort); } catch {} resolve({ id, ...r }); };
+      const timer = setTimeout(() => { fileLog(`plugin ${pluginName}: request ${id} had no answer in ${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s`); end({ replied: false, timedOut: true }); }, ms);
+      try { timer.unref?.(); } catch {}
+      _pluginReplies.set(id, { plugin: pluginName, end, timer });
+      if (signal) { if (signal.aborted) { onAbort(); return; } signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true }); }
+      wakeAgent(`${head}\n${text}${tail}`, { logId: `plugin ${pluginName} ${id}`, source: 'plugin', plugin: meta });
+    });
+  }
+  /** replyTo(id, text) in the agent's snippet: the answer to a plugin's request goes back to it. Returns what to tell the agent. */
+  async function pluginReplyFromAgent(id, text) {
+    const k = String(id ?? '').trim();
+    const e = _pluginReplies.get(k);
+    if (!e) {
+      const open = [..._pluginReplies.keys()];
+      throw new Error(`replyTo("${k}"): no request ${k} is waiting${open.length ? ` - the ones waiting are ${open.join(', ')}` : ' - none is (answered already, timed out, or its plugin was reloaded)'}`);
+    }
+    let out = String(text == null ? '' : text);
+    if (filterPipeline.active()) out = await filterPipeline.unmask(out, { hook: 'tool-args', toolName: 'replyTo', projectDir: session.activeProject || undefined });
+    e.end({ replied: true, text: out });

+    return `sent to ${e.plugin} as its answer to request ${k}`;

+// A WRITE HERE: the copy changed at once (and the version), then the disk - one write at a time for a file, in the
+// order they were made. If the disk refuses it, the copy of that file is thrown away and read again from disk.
+function vaultLocalChange(rel, text, toDisk) {
+  vaultLocalRoot();
+  const st = vaultLocalState();
+  vaultLocalLoad(st, rel);   // (held before it changes: a read must never fall back to a disk the write has not reached)
+  st.files.set(rel, text);
+  vaultListSet(st, rel, text !== null);
+  vaultLocalBump(st, rel);
+  const seq = ++st.wseq;
+  st.wrote.set(rel, seq);
+  st.busy.set(rel, (st.busy.get(rel) || 0) + 1);
+  const p = vaultLocalPath(rel);
+  const run = (st.chains.get(rel) || Promise.resolve()).then(async () => {
+    try {
+      await toDisk(p);
+      try { st.sig.set(rel, vaultSigOf(await stat(p))); } catch { st.sig.delete(rel); }
+    } catch (e) {
+      if (_vl === st) { st.files.delete(rel); st.sig.delete(rel); vaultLocalBump(st, rel); }
+      throw e;
+    } finally {
+      st.wrote.set(rel, ++st.wseq);   // (done during a look at the disk: that look's view of it is older than this)
+      const n = (st.busy.get(rel) || 1) - 1;
+      if (n > 0) st.busy.set(rel, n); else st.busy.delete(rel);
+    }
+  });
+  const tail = run.catch(() => {});
+  st.chains.set(rel, tail);
+  tail.then(() => { if (st.chains.get(rel) === tail) st.chains.delete(rel); });
+  return run.then(() => ({ ok: true }));

+/**
+ * WHAT `javap` PRINTS (prod, 2026-11-18: `javap -c` came back with every JVM instruction, the type descriptors and the
+ * Java keywords masked - aload_0, invokevirtual, Ljava/lang/Object;, public, extends). It is code: read as Java is, and
+ * javap's own notes after // (Field x:Ljava/..;, Method a/b.c:()V) are code too - except the constant of an ldc,
+ * "// String …", and a constant pool's Utf8 entry, which are a program's string literals and judged as strings are.
+ */
+const JAVAP_COMPILED = /^Compiled from "[^"\r\n]*"[ \t]*\r?$/m;
+const JAVAP_CODE = /^[ \t]+Code:[ \t]*\r?$/m;
+const JAVAP_INSN = /^[ \t]+\d+: [a-z][a-z0-9_]*(?:[ \t]|\r?$)/m;
+export const looksLikeJavap = (t) => JAVAP_COMPILED.test(t) || (JAVAP_CODE.test(t) && JAVAP_INSN.test(t));
+function lexJavap(L, from, to) {
+  const t = L.t;
+  lexCLike(L, from, to);
+  let i = from;
+  while (i < to) {
+    const eol = lineEnd(t, i, to);
+    const line = t.slice(i, eol);
+    const utf8 = /^\s*#\d+ = Utf8 /.exec(line);
+    if (utf8) { L.fill(i, i + utf8[0].length, CODE); L.fill(i + utf8[0].length, eol, STRING); }
+    else {
+      const c = line.indexOf('//');
+      if (c >= 0) {
+        const str = /^\/\/\s*String /.exec(line.slice(c));
+        L.fill(i + c, eol, CODE);
+        if (str) L.fill(i + c + str[0].length, eol, STRING);
+      }
+    }
+    i = eol + 1;
+  }

=== add test/test-bookkeeper-keeps-up.mjs
new 55594667abf31e05680eb615
+/**
+ * THE BOOKKEEPER KEEPS UP DURING A RUN (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md section 4; the developer, 2026-11-18: "the bookkeeper doesn't
+ * run very often, so the checklist is out of sync ... running anytime there's messages it hasn't processed yet,
+ * instead of waiting until the run ends or 10 messages. The heads up agent could wait").
+ * Through the real server, a scripted agent of 8 exchanges, each a moment long (CB_BK_SETTLE_MS short):
+ *   [1] the bookkeeper's passes run during the run, from the first exchanges - long before an exchange 10
+ *   [2] never two at once, and none asked for with nothing new (no call then)
+ *   [3] Heads up does not run mid-run in a run shorter than MID_RUN_EVERY: it waits for the end, as before
+ *   [4] the end of the run still has its own pass
+ * Run: node test/test-bookkeeper-keeps-up.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-keepup-home-'));
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER = '0';
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+process.env.CB_BK_SETTLE_MS = '150';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-keepup-proj-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'notes.txt'), 'notes\n');
+let turns = 0, running = false, inBk = 0, maxBk = 0;
+const PLAN = 8;
+const side = [];
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const c = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{3,8}) ---\s*$/.exec(c) || [])[1] || 'zzzz';
+    turns++;
+    await sleep(350);   // (an exchange takes a moment: the bookkeeper has time to keep up between them)
+    const text = turns < PLAN ? `Step ${turns}.\n<remote-workspace><js>await write("step-${turns}.txt", "step ${turns}\n"); return "wrote step ${turns}";</js></remote-workspace>` : `All done.\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: text } }] };
+  },
+  async complete(req) {
+    const text = (req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n');
+    const who = /Bring the record up to date/.test(text) ? 'bookkeeper' : /heads.?up/i.test(text) ? 'heads up' : 'other';
+    side.push({ who, atTurn: turns, running });
+    if (who === 'bookkeeper') { inBk++; maxBk = Math.max(maxBk, inBk); await sleep(200); inBk--; }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: who === 'bookkeeper' ? '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' : 'NOTHING' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const PORT = 29480 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30), B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+const post = async (p, body) => (await fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })).json().catch(() => ({}));
+const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json().catch(() => ({}));
+const logOf = async () => readFile(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'code-boss.log'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+try {
+  await sleep(800);
+  await post('/api/agent-flags', { flag: 'bookkeeper', on: true });
+  console.log('\n[1] a run of 8 exchanges');
+  running = true;
+  const watch = (async () => { while (running) { running = (await state()).status === 'running'; await sleep(50); } })();
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Go through the steps, please.', model: 'm' });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running' && i > 3) break; await sleep(100); }
+  running = false; await watch;
+  await sleep(1500);
+  const log = await logOf();
+  // (during the run: called before the agent's last exchange)
+  const mid = side.filter((x) => x.who === 'bookkeeper' && x.atTurn < PLAN);
+  ok(turns === PLAN, `the agent took ${PLAN} exchanges (${turns})`);
+  ok(mid.length >= 2, `the bookkeeper's passes ran during the run (${mid.length}, at turns ${mid.map((x) => x.atTurn).join(', ')})`);
+  ok(mid.length && mid[0].atTurn <= 3, `from the first exchanges - not waiting for exchange 10 (first at turn ${mid.length ? mid[0].atTurn : '-'})`);
+  ok(/bookkeeper: pass \(keeping up during the run\)/.test(log), 'the log names them');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] one at a time');
+  ok(maxBk === 1, `never two bookkeeper calls at once (${maxBk})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] Heads up waits');
+  const huMid = side.filter((x) => x.who === 'heads up' && x.atTurn < PLAN);
+  ok(huMid.length === 0 && !/mid-run agents/.test(log), `no Heads up during a run shorter than ten exchanges (${huMid.length})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the end of the run');
+  ok(/bookkeeper: pass \(end of the run\)/.test(log) && side.some((x) => x.who === 'heads up' && x.atTurn === PLAN), 'its own pass, and Heads up after it');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { try { server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {} server.close(); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-conversation-patch-lines.mjs
new 8fa0e53462975f70d5f88088
+/**
+ * A PATCH TO A SAVED EVENT IS A LINE, NOT A REWRITE (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md 6.1; the inventory of 2026-11-18: the call's id
+ * stamped on the user's message after it was flushed made most turns rewrite the whole conversation.jsonl, on the one
+ * event loop every window shares):
+ *   [1] a run: the stamp on the already-saved user message is appended as { cbPatch, set } - no full rewrite (no .bak)
+ *   [2] a restart reads it back: the message carries its call's id, every event once
+ *   [3] an older reader (lines with no id skipped) still reads every event
+ *   [4] many patches: past CB_CV_PATCH_LINES_MAX the file is rewritten whole once, and reads the same
+ * Run: node test/test-conversation-patch-lines.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cvpatch-home-'));
+process.env.CB_CV_PATCH_LINES_MAX = '3';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PORT = 29740 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40), B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const proj = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cvpatch-proj-'));
+const jsonl = path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl');
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { await sleep(500); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Done here.' } }] }; },   // (after the message's own flush, 300 ms)
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};
+const opts = () => ({ html: '<html><head></head></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+const post = async (p, body) => (await fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })).json().catch(() => ({}));
+const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=500')).json();
+const settle = async () => { await sleep(400); for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running') { await sleep(700); return s; } await sleep(150); } };
+const lines = () => readFileSync(jsonl, 'utf8').split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim()).map((l) => JSON.parse(l));
+
+let srv = await startServer(opts());
+try {
+  await sleep(600);
+  console.log('\n[1] a run');
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'please say hello', model: 'm' });
+  await settle();
+  const ls = lines();
+  const patches = ls.filter((l) => l.cbPatch);
+  const user = ls.find((l) => l.type === 'user-message');
+  ok(patches.length >= 1 && patches.some((p) => p.cbPatch === user.id && p.set && p.set.rawId), `the stamp on the saved message is a line (${patches.length}: ${JSON.stringify(patches[0] || null).slice(0, 120)})`);
+  ok(!existsSync(jsonl + '.bak'), 'no full rewrite happened (no .bak)');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a restart');
+  const before = (await state()).events || [];
+  await new Promise((r) => srv.server.close(r));
+  srv = await startServer(opts());
+  await sleep(800);
+  const after = (await state()).events || [];
+  const u = after.find((e) => e.type === 'user-message');
+  ok(u && u.rawId && u.rawId === (before.find((e) => e.id === u.id) || {}).rawId, `the message carries its call's id after a restart (${u && u.rawId})`);
+  ok(after.length === before.length && new Set(after.map((e) => e.id)).size === after.length, `every event once (${before.length} -> ${after.length})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] an older reader');
+  const old = readFileSync(jsonl, 'utf8').split('\n').map((l) => { try { const e = JSON.parse(l); return e && e.id ? e : null; } catch { return null; } }).filter(Boolean);
+  ok(old.length === after.length, `lines with an id: every event (${old.length})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] many patches');
+  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { await post('/api/message', { text: 'again ' + i, model: 'm' }); await settle(); }
+  const final = (await state()).events || [];
+  ok(existsSync(jsonl + '.bak'), 'past the limit the file was rewritten whole once (a .bak)');
+  const re = lines();
+  const evs = re.filter((l) => l.id);
+  ok(evs.length === final.length && re.filter((l) => l.cbPatch).length <= 3, `and it holds every event, few patch lines (${evs.length} events, ${re.filter((l) => l.cbPatch).length} patches)`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { try { srv.server.closeAllConnections?.(); srv.server.close(); } catch {} }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-host-control.mjs
new 1889b541d5a90e674c5e1744
+/**
+ * THE HOST'S CONTROL PORT (src/host.mjs; ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md "Host control API"): what the container's manager and
+ * the launcher use to start and stop windows' servers in the one process.
+ *   [1] no key, a wrong key: refused (403); the key is out of the environment
+ *   [2] POST /host/servers: a window server listens on the port asked for; health lists it, with the host's own pid
+ *   [3] a second window: two servers; the same port twice is refused (409)
+ *   [4] DELETE: that window's server stops - its conversation saved - and the other goes on
+ *   [5] POST /host/quit: every server closes, the process would end (exit 0)
+ * Run: node test/test-host-control.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-hostctl-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOST_KEY = 'k'.repeat(32);
+const { startServer, cbSetHostMode } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { runHost, hostWindowEnv } = await import('../src/host.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const KEY = 'k'.repeat(32);
+const BASE = 29700 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40) * 2;
+const html = '<!doctype html><html><head></head><body>code_boss</body></html>';
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; } };
+let exited = null;
+const host = await runHost({
+  startServer, setHostMode: cbSetHostMode, controlPort: 0, build: 'test', homeDir: process.env.CB_HOME, idleMs: 0, log: () => {},
+  serverOpts: { html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }] },
+  exit: (code) => { exited = code; },
+});
+const H = `http://127.0.0.1:${host.port}`;
+const call = (method, p, body, key = KEY) => fetch(H + p, { method, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', ...(key ? { 'x-cb-host-key': key } : {}) }, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) }).then(async (r) => ({ status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }));
+const health = async () => (await call('GET', '/host/health')).body;
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] the key');
+  ok((await call('GET', '/host/health', undefined, null)).status === 403, 'no key: 403');
+  ok((await call('GET', '/host/health', undefined, 'x'.repeat(32))).status === 403, 'a wrong key of the same length: 403');
+  ok(process.env.CB_HOST_KEY === undefined, 'the key is out of the environment (no process code_boss starts inherits it)');
+  ok(existsSync(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'host.json')) && JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'host.json'), 'utf8')).port === host.port, 'host.json says where it listens');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a window server');
+  let r = await call('POST', '/host/servers', { port: BASE, instanceId: 2, restartKey: 'ab'.repeat(16), managerUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:1234' });
+  ok(r.status === 200 && r.body.id && r.body.port === BASE, `started (${r.status} ${JSON.stringify(r.body)})`);
+  const id1 = r.body.id;
+  const h = await health();
+  ok(h.pid === process.pid && h.servers.length === 1 && h.servers[0].id === id1 && h.servers[0].port === BASE, `health lists it, with the host's own pid (${JSON.stringify(h.servers)})`);
+  const page = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${BASE}/`)).text();
+  ok(page.includes('window.CB_RESTART=') && page.includes('ab'.repeat(16)), 'its own restart key reaches its page');
+  ok((await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${BASE}/api/health`)).json()).ok, 'it answers as a code_boss');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a second window');
+  r = await call('POST', '/host/servers', { port: BASE + 1 });
+  ok(r.status === 200 && r.body.id !== id1, `a second server (${JSON.stringify(r.body)})`);
+  const id2 = r.body.id;
+  ok((await health()).servers.length === 2, 'health lists both');
+  ok((await call('POST', '/host/servers', { port: BASE + 1 })).status === 409, 'the same port again: 409');
+  ok((await call('POST', '/host/servers', {})).status === 400, 'no port: 400');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] one window closes');
+  const proj = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-hostctl-proj-'));
+  await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${BASE}/api/open-project`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ path: proj }) });
+  r = await call('DELETE', `/host/servers/${id1}?why=its%20window%20closed`);
+  ok(r.status === 200 && r.body.closed === id1, `closed (${JSON.stringify(r.body)})`);
+  const after = await health();
+  ok(after.servers.length === 1 && after.servers[0].id === id2, 'the other is still listed');
+  ok((await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${BASE + 1}/api/health`)).json()).ok, 'and still answers');
+  let gone = false; try { await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${BASE}/api/health`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(1500) }); } catch { gone = true; }
+  ok(gone, 'the closed one no longer answers');
+  ok(!existsSync(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'instance.lock')), 'its project\'s lock is released');
+  ok((await call('DELETE', '/host/servers/nope')).status === 404, 'an unknown id: 404');
+
+  console.log('\n[5] quit');
+  r = await call('POST', '/host/quit?why=test');
+  ok(r.status === 200, 'answered');
+  for (let i = 0; i < 60 && exited === null; i++) await sleep(100);
+  ok(exited === 0 && host.servers.size === 0, `every server closed, exit 0 (${exited}, ${host.servers.size} left)`);
+  ok(!existsSync(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'host.json')), 'host.json removed');
+

new 56aa45d5c96ae529cfa80fd0
+/**
+ * SEVERAL WINDOW SERVERS IN ONE PROCESS (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md, the developer, 2026-11-18: "one process with multiple
+ * servers ... just one instance"). Each window's server is a startServer() of its own, in this one process:
+ *   [1] two servers, two ports, two projects: each answers with its own
+ *   [2] a project open in one window is refused in the other ("open in another window"); free again once it closes
+ *   [3] a turn is logged in its own window's project only
+ *   [4] a window's own values (what was per-process environment): its restart key reaches its page, not the other's
+ *   [5] closing one server leaves the other working, and its lock is gone
+ *   [6] in a host, an uncaught error is logged and the other windows go on
+ * Run: node test/test-host-multi-server.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-multi-home-'));
+const { startServer, cbSetHostMode, cbLiveServers } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const BASE = 29610 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30) * 3;
+const [PA, PB, PC] = [BASE, BASE + 1, BASE + 2];
+const projA = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-multi-a-')), projB = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-multi-b-'));
+const html = '<!doctype html><html><head></head><body>code_boss</body></html>';
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { for (const p of ['Done', ' here.']) { await sleep(2); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: p } }] }; } },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};
+const opts = (port, extra = {}) => ({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], ...extra });
+const api = (port, p, body) => fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}${p}`, body === undefined ? {} : { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }).then(async (r) => ({ status: r.status, body: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }));
+const health = async (port) => (await api(port, '/api/health')).body;
+const settle = async (port) => { await sleep(400); for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { const s = (await api(port, '/api/state?limit=50')).body; if (s.status !== 'running') return s; await sleep(150); } };
+
+cbSetHostMode(true);
+const a = await startServer(opts(PA));
+const b = await startServer(opts(PB));
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] two servers in one process');
+  ok(a.id && b.id && a.id !== b.id && cbLiveServers().length === 2, `two live servers (${a.id}, ${b.id})`);
+  let r = await api(PA, '/api/open-project', { path: projA });
+  ok(r.status === 200, `window A opens project A (${r.status} ${r.body.error || ''})`);
+  r = await api(PB, '/api/open-project', { path: projB });
+  ok(r.status === 200, `window B opens project B (${r.status} ${r.body.error || ''})`);
+  const [ha, hb] = [await health(PA), await health(PB)];
+  ok(path.resolve(ha.project) === path.resolve(projA) && path.resolve(hb.project) === path.resolve(projB), 'each answers with its own project');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] one project, one window');
+  r = await api(PB, '/api/open-project', { path: projA });
+  ok(r.status === 400 && r.body.locked, `window B is refused project A, open in window A (${r.status} ${r.body.error || ''})`);
+  const lock = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(projA, '.code_boss', 'instance.lock'), 'utf8'));
+  ok(lock.pid === process.pid && lock.server === a.id && lock.port === PA, `the lock names window A's server (${JSON.stringify(lock)})`);
+  ok(path.resolve((await health(PB)).project) === path.resolve(projB), 'window B still has project B');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a turn goes to its own window\'s log');
+  await api(PB, '/api/message', { text: 'hello', model: 'm' });
+  await settle(PB);
+  const turnsA = path.join(projA, '.code_boss', 'turns.jsonl'), turnsB = path.join(projB, '.code_boss', 'turns.jsonl');
+  ok(existsSync(turnsB) && !existsSync(turnsA), `B's turn in B's turns.jsonl only (B ${existsSync(turnsB)}, A ${existsSync(turnsA)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] a window\'s own values');
+  const key = 'ab'.repeat(16);
+  const c = await startServer(opts(PC, { windowEnv: { CB_MANAGER_URL: 'http://127.0.0.1:1234', CB_RESTART_KEY: key } }));
+  const pageC = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PC}/`)).text(), pageA = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PA}/`)).text();
+  ok(pageC.includes('window.CB_RESTART=') && pageC.includes(key), 'its restart key reaches its own page');
+  ok(!pageA.includes('window.CB_RESTART='), 'and not the other window\'s');
+  await new Promise((res) => c.server.close(res));
+
+  console.log('\n[5] closing one window\'s server');
+  await new Promise((res) => a.server.close(res));
+  ok(cbLiveServers().length === 1 && cbLiveServers()[0].id === b.id, 'one server left, window B\'s');
+  ok(!existsSync(path.join(projA, '.code_boss', 'instance.lock')), 'window A\'s lock is gone');

+  ok(s && s.status !== 'running' && (await health(PB)).ok, 'window B still runs and answers');
+
+  console.log('\n[6] an uncaught error in a host');
+  setTimeout(() => { throw new Error('a test error, thrown on purpose'); }, 1);
+  await sleep(300);
+  ok((await health(PB)).ok, 'the process went on: window B answers');
+  const diag = readFileSync(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'diag.log'), 'utf8');
+  ok(/kept running: 1 window\(s\) open/.test(diag) && /a test error, thrown on purpose/.test(diag), 'logged, and said it kept running');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally {
+  for (const sv of [a, b]) { try { await new Promise((res) => sv.server.close(res)); } catch {} }

+/**
+ * EACH WINDOW SEES ONLY ITS OWN PROJECT (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md; the developer, 2026-11-18: "this is something to test to
+ * make sure each instance is only seeing its own project"). Two window servers in this one process, each with its own
+ * project and its own model adapter, both running at the same moment (with the bookkeeper keeping up beside them).
+ * Every message, reply and background process of window A carries the word ALPHAQX, of window B the word BRAVOQX; each
+ * window is then searched for the other's word everywhere it can show or keep something:
+ *   [1] what its model was sent (every call: the agent's, the bookkeeper's, the rest)
+ *   [2] its state (/api/state: the chat, the checklist, everything the page is given) and its events page
+ *   [3] its live stream (/api/stream, open while the other window worked)
+ *   [4] its model-call views (/api/llm-calls, /api/llm-activity)
+ *   [5] its background processes (/api/bg), with one started in the other window's project
+ *   [6] every file under its project's .code_boss folder (conversation, logs, raw log, turns, the bookkeeper's record)

+let pass = 0, fail = 0;

+// a live stream, kept as text

+  s.req = http.get({ host: '127.0.0.1', port, path: '/api/stream' }, (res) => { res.setEncoding('utf8'); res.on('data', (d) => { s.text += d; }); res.on('error', () => {}); });
+  s.req.on('error', () => {});
+  return s;
+};
+const filesUnder = (dir) => {
+  const out = [];
+  const walk = (d) => { let names = []; try { names = readdirSync(d); } catch { return; } for (const n of names) { const p = path.join(d, n); let st; try { st = statSync(p); } catch { continue; } if (st.isDirectory()) walk(p); else if (st.size < 20e6) out.push(p); } };
+  walk(dir);
+  return out;
+};
+
+cbSetHostMode(true);
+W.A.srv = await startServer(opts(W.A));
+W.B.srv = await startServer(opts(W.B));

+    await Promise.all(['A', 'B'].map((k) => settle(W[k].port)));
+  }

+  ok(bg && (bg.id || bg.ok !== false), `a background process started in B's project (${JSON.stringify(bg).slice(0, 120)})`);
+  await sleep(1500);   // (the bookkeeper's last pass, the stream's bg-procs push)

+    ok(w.sent.length >= 3 && sent.includes(w.word), `[1] its model was sent its own work (${w.sent.length} calls)`);
+    ok(!sent.includes(o.word), `[1] and nothing of window ${k === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A'}'s`);
+    const st = (await api(w.port, '/api/state?limit=500')).text, ev = (await api(w.port, '/api/events?limit=500')).text;
+    ok(st.includes(w.word) && !st.includes(o.word), `[2] its state has its own word only (own ${st.includes(w.word)}, other ${st.includes(o.word)})`);
+    ok(ev.includes(w.word) && !ev.includes(o.word), `[2] its events page too (own ${ev.includes(w.word)}, other ${ev.includes(o.word)})`);
+    ok(w.stream.text.includes(w.word) && !w.stream.text.includes(o.word), `[3] its live stream (${w.stream.text.length} chars; own ${w.stream.text.includes(w.word)}, other ${w.stream.text.includes(o.word)})`);
+    const calls = (await api(w.port, '/api/llm-calls')).text, act = (await api(w.port, '/api/llm-activity')).text;
+    ok(act.includes(w.word) && !act.includes(o.word) && !calls.includes(o.word), `[4] its model-call views (own ${act.includes(w.word)}, other ${act.includes(o.word) || calls.includes(o.word)})`);
+    const bgList = (await api(w.port, '/api/bg')).body.processes || [];
+    if (k === 'B') ok(bgList.some((p) => String(p.label || '').includes(W.B.word)), `[5] B lists its background process (${bgList.length})`);
+    else ok(!bgList.length, `[5] A lists none of B's background processes (${bgList.length})`);
+    const files = filesUnder(path.join(w.proj, '.code_boss'));
+    const leaks = files.filter((f) => { try { return readFileSync(f, 'latin1').includes(o.word); } catch { return false; } });
+    const own = files.filter((f) => { try { return readFileSync(f, 'latin1').includes(w.word); } catch { return false; } });
+    ok(own.length > 0, `[6] its project folder keeps its own work (${own.map((f) => path.relative(w.proj, f)).join(', ')})`);
+    ok(!leaks.length, `[6] and no file in it has the other window's (${files.length} files${leaks.length ? '; LEAK in ' + leaks.map((f) => path.relative(w.proj, f)).join(', ') : ''})`);
+  }
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally {

+  for (const w of [W.A, W.B]) { try { w.stream?.req?.destroy(); } catch {} try { await new Promise((res) => w.srv.server.close(res)); } catch {} }

+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

+/**
+ * ONE QUEUE FOR EVERY WINDOW'S MODEL CALLS (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md section 3; the developer, 2026-11-18, six windows open
+ * and each window's budget blind to the others): two window servers in one process share one count of the minute.

+    const who = /Bring the record up to date/.test(text) ? (/window-b/.test(text) ? 'bookkeeper B' : 'bookkeeper A') : 'other';
+    calls.push({ who, at: Date.now() });
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: /bookkeeper/.test(who) ? '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' : '' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};

+
+cbSetHostMode(true);
+const a = await startServer(opts(BASE, projA)), b = await startServer(opts(BASE + 1, projB));
+try {
+  await sleep(800);
+  for (const p of [BASE, BASE + 1]) await post(p, '/api/agent-flags', { flag: 'bookkeeper', on: true });
+  // (tiny: one agent call spends it - and it is the process's minute: set in one window, it holds for both)

+  await post(BASE, '/api/message', { text: 'window-a: add a delete subcommand, please', model: 'm' });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !calls.some((c) => c.who === 'agent A'); i++) await sleep(50);
+  await sleep(300);
+  await post(BASE + 1, '/api/message', { text: 'window-b: and a list subcommand here', model: 'm' });

+  const agA = calls.find((c) => c.who === 'agent A'), agB = calls.find((c) => c.who === 'agent B'), bkA = calls.find((c) => c.who === 'bookkeeper A');
+  ok(agA && agA.at - t0 < 3000, `A's agent went at once (${agA && agA.at - t0} ms)`);

+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }

new 12f82c298013d55aed8e5d63
+/**
+ * ONE QUEUE FOR EVERY MODEL CALL (src/llm/scheduler.mjs; ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md section 3). A fake clock and a fake sleep
+ * that counts its live timers - deterministic, no real waiting except [R] (one real second).
+ *   [1] level 0 never waits, only counts            [2] levels 1-3 go at once with room
+ *   [3] level order, oldest first within a level     [4] round-robin across windows
+ *   [5] the reserve formula                          [6] the reserve melts: a level-3 call goes under constant level-0 load
+ *   [7] maxWait: past it a call goes whatever the count
+ *   [8] requests a minute                            [9] the background in-flight cap
+ *   [10] completion tokens counted at release, at the time the call went
+ *   [11] a stopped call stops waiting                [12] a 429: the hold, and learning a limit
+ *   [13] the gateway's reading raises the count      [14] a call bigger than the whole minute
+ *   [15] limits changed while a call waits           [16] per model
+ *   [17] no timer or listener left behind after many waits, and no polling
+ *   [18] AsyncLocalStorage context survives a wait   [19] snapshot          [20] close()
+ *   [R] the real timer
+ * Run: node test/test-llm-scheduler.mjs
+ */
+import { AsyncLocalStorage } from 'node:async_hooks';
+import { getEventListeners } from 'node:events';
+import { createScheduler, schReserve, schLevel, SCH_PATIENCE_MS, SCH_MAX_WAIT_MS, SCH_WINDOW_MS } from '../src/llm/scheduler.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// a fake clock: sleep(ms, signal) parks a timer (taken off when the signal aborts); advance() moves time timer by timer
+function fakeClock(start = 1_000_000) {
+  let t = start;
+  const timers = new Set();
+  let sleeps = 0;
+  const flush = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r)); };
+  const sleep = (ms, signal) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+    sleeps++;
+    if (signal && signal.aborted) { resolve(); return; }
+    const tm = { at: t + Math.max(0, ms), resolve, signal, off: null };
+    timers.add(tm);
+    if (signal) { tm.off = () => { timers.delete(tm); resolve(); }; signal.addEventListener('abort', tm.off, { once: true }); }
+  });
+  const fire = (tm) => { timers.delete(tm); if (tm.signal && tm.off) tm.signal.removeEventListener('abort', tm.off); tm.resolve(); };
+  return {
+    now: () => t,
+    sleep,
+    flush,
+    active: () => timers.size,
+    sleeps: () => sleeps,
+    async advanceTo(target) {
+      await flush();
+      for (let guard = 0; guard < 100000; guard++) {
+        let next = null;
+        for (const tm of timers) if (tm.at <= target && (!next || tm.at < next.at)) next = tm;
+        if (!next) break;
+        t = Math.max(t, next.at);
+        fire(next);
+        await flush();
+      }
+      t = Math.max(t, target);
+      await flush();
+    },
+    advance(ms) { return this.advanceTo(t + ms); },
+    /** advance until done() or `max` ms pass; returns done() */
+    async until(done, max) {
+      const end = t + max;
+      await flush();
+      while (!done() && t < end) {
+        let next = null;
+        for (const tm of timers) if (!next || tm.at < next.at) next = tm;
+        if (!next || next.at > end) { t = end; await flush(); break; }
+        t = Math.max(t, next.at);
+        fire(next);
+        await flush();
+      }
+      return done();
+    },
+  };
+}
+const mk = (clock, opts = {}) => createScheduler({ now: clock.now, sleep: clock.sleep, ...opts });
+// a call waiting: { ticket, err, at } filled in as it settles
+const track = (s, req, clock) => {
+  const r = { ticket: null, err: null, at: null };
+  s.admit(req).then((tk) => { r.ticket = tk; r.at = clock.now(); }, (e) => { r.err = e; r.at = clock.now(); });
+  return r;
+};
+

+  await s.admit({ level: 1, model: 'other', tokens: 1 });   // (the one background slot taken)

+console.log('\n[12] a 429: the hold, and learning a limit');
+{
+  const c = fakeClock();

+  await c.until(() => r2.ticket, 200000);
+  // the hold ends at 20 s; then the learned count (1000 + 1 + 1 in the minute, against 900) holds it until the window frees
+  ok(r2.ticket && r2.ticket.waited >= 20000, `it was held at least until the 429's wait was over (${r2.ticket && r2.ticket.waited} ms)`);
+  const c2 = fakeClock();
+  const s2 = mk(c2, { limitsFor: () => ({ tokensPerMinute: 1e9, requestsPerMinute: 1e9 }) });
+  s2.note('m', 500);
+  s2.throttled('m', 100000);
+  ok(s2.snapshot().models.m.learned.tokensPerMinute === 0, 'a model with a limit in Settings learns nothing');
+  const h1 = track(s2, { level: 1, model: 'm' }, c2), h3 = track(s2, { level: 3, model: 'm' }, c2);
+  await c2.until(() => h1.ticket && h3.ticket, 200000);
+  ok(h1.ticket && h1.ticket.waited === SCH_PATIENCE_MS[1] && h3.ticket && h3.ticket.waited === SCH_PATIENCE_MS[3], `a 100 s hold is bounded by each level's patience (level 1 went at ${h1.ticket && h1.ticket.waited}, level 3 at ${h3.ticket && h3.ticket.waited})`);
+  const c3 = fakeClock();
+  const s3 = mk(c3);
+  s3.note('m', 1000); s3.throttled('m', 1000);
+  s3.note('m', 9000); s3.throttled('m', 1000);
+  ok(s3.limitsOf('m').tokensPerMinute === 900, 'a learned limit only comes down (a later 429 at more does not raise it)');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[13] the gateway\'s reading raises the count');
+{

+console.log('\n[17] no timer or listener left behind after many waits, and no polling');
+{
+  const c = fakeClock();
+  const s = mk(c, { limitsFor: () => ({ tokensPerMinute: 1000 }), backgroundInflight: 3 });
+  const ac = new AbortController();   // one long-lived signal across every wait (the upgrade's, across its chunks)
+  let done = 0, aborted = 0;
+  const all = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
+    if (i % 10 === 0) s.note('m', 900);
+    const own = i % 7 === 0 ? new AbortController() : null;
+    const p = s.admit({ level: 1 + (i % 3), model: 'm', tokens: 50, server: `w${i % 4}`, signal: own ? own.signal : ac.signal })
+      .then((tk) => { done++; return c.sleep(500).then(() => tk.release({ completionTokens: 10 })); }, () => { aborted++; });
+    all.push(p);
+    if (own && i % 2 === 0) setImmediate(() => own.abort());
+    await c.advance(300);
+  }
+  await c.until(() => done + aborted === 200, 3_600_000);
+  await c.advance(1000);   // (the last calls' own 500 ms, then their release)
+  await Promise.all(all);
+  await c.flush();
+  ok(done + aborted === 200 && aborted > 0, `all 200 settled (${done} went, ${aborted} stopped)`);
+  ok(c.active() === 0, `no timer left (${c.active()})`);
+  ok(getEventListeners(ac.signal, 'abort').length === 0, `no listener left on the shared signal (${getEventListeners(ac.signal, 'abort').length})`);
+  ok(s.snapshot().waiting === 0 && s.snapshot().background.inflight === 0, 'nobody waiting, nothing in flight');
+  // polling: one call waiting its longest wait sets a handful of timers, not one a second
+  const c2 = fakeClock();
+  const s2 = mk(c2, { limitsFor: () => ({ tokensPerMinute: 1000 }) });
+  s2.note('m', 5000);
+  const before = c2.sleeps();
+  const r = track(s2, { level: 3, model: 'm', tokens: 100 }, c2);
+  await c2.until(() => r.ticket, 400000);
+  ok(r.ticket && c2.sleeps() - before <= 3, `a ${r.ticket && r.ticket.waited / 1000} s wait took ${c2.sleeps() - before} timer(s) - it is woken, it does not poll`);
+}
+

=== add test/test-mermaid-labels.mjs
new b323a77583d1e0c102818bdc
+/**
+ * DIAGRAM LABELS (mermaid-lite.mjs), 2026-11-18:
+ *   [1] "<br/>" in a label is a line break (the developer: "in diagrams the agent puts "<br/>" for a line break but we're
+ *       not rendering"): one tspan a line, the box sized for the widest line and the number of lines
+ *   [2] <b>/<strong>, <i>/<em>, <u> style their words; any other tag is taken out, its words kept; nothing from a label
+ *       reaches the page as markup (a <script> or an onerror is text)
+ *   [3] connector labels do not cover each other (the developer: "sometimes the connector labels overlap each other"),
+ *       nor a box - four labelled arrows out of one node
+ *   [4] an arrow with its label written into it draws (A -- yes --> B), as the |label| form does
+ *   [5] sequence diagrams and artifact titles read a label as one line of words
+ *   [6] a label full of tags renders at once (a shared regex once looped forever)
+ * Run: node test/test-mermaid-labels.mjs
+ */
+import { mmRender, mmParse, mmPlainLabel } from '../src/mermaid-lite.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const rects = (svg, cls) => [...svg.matchAll(new RegExp(`<g class="${cls}"><rect x="([-\\d.]+)" y="([-\\d.]+)" width="([\\d.]+)" height="([\\d.]+)"`, 'g'))].map((m) => ({ x1: +m[1], y1: +m[2], x2: +m[1] + +m[3], y2: +m[2] + +m[4] }));
+const overlap = (a, b) => Math.max(0, Math.min(a.x2, b.x2) - Math.max(a.x1, b.x1)) * Math.max(0, Math.min(a.y2, b.y2) - Math.max(a.y1, b.y1));
+
+console.log('\n[1] line breaks');
+{
+  const one = mmRender('flowchart TD\n  A[Order placed]'), two = mmRender('flowchart TD\n  A["Order<br/>placed"]');
+  ok(!/&lt;br/.test(two) && (two.match(/<tspan x=/g) || []).length === 2, 'a <br/> splits the label into two lines (no "<br/>" shown)');
+  const h = (svg) => +(svg.match(/<rect x="[-\d.]+" y="[-\d.]+" width="[\d.]+" height="([\d.]+)"/) || [])[1];
+  const w = (svg) => +(svg.match(/<rect x="[-\d.]+" y="[-\d.]+" width="([\d.]+)"/) || [])[1];
+  ok(h(two) > h(one) && w(two) < w(one), `the box is taller and narrower for two lines (${w(one)}x${h(one)} -> ${w(two)}x${h(two)})`);
+  for (const br of ['<br>', '<br/>', '<br />', '<BR>']) ok((mmRender(`flowchart TD\n  A["a${br}b"]`).match(/<tspan x=/g) || []).length === 2, `${br} is a break`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] simple markup, and nothing else');
+{
+  const svg = mmRender('flowchart TD\n  A["<b>Ship</b> <i>it</i> <u>now</u>"] --> B["<span class=x>kept</span> words"]');
+  ok(/font-weight="700">Ship</.test(svg) && /font-style="italic">it</.test(svg) && /text-decoration="underline">now</.test(svg), 'bold, italic and underline style their words');
+  ok(/kept/.test(svg) && !/span class/.test(svg) && !/&lt;span/.test(svg), 'another tag is taken out, its words kept');
+  // (on a connector's label too - the developer, 2026-11-18: "do we support html tags in the connector labels")
+  const conn = mmRender('flowchart TD\n  A[Start] -- "<b>yes</b><br/>after <i>review</i>" --> B[Ship]');
+  const connLabel = (conn.match(/<g class="mm-edge-label">[\s\S]*?<\/g>/) || [''])[0];
+  ok(/font-weight="700">yes</.test(connLabel) && /font-style="italic">review</.test(connLabel) && (connLabel.match(/<tspan x=/g) || []).length === 2 && !/&lt;/.test(connLabel), 'a connector\'s label: bold, italic and a line break, no tag shown');
+  const evil = mmRender('flowchart TD\n  A["<script>alert(1)</script>"] --> B["<img src=x onerror=alert(2)>x"]');
+  ok(!/<script|onerror|<img/i.test(evil), 'a <script> or an <img onerror> reaches the page as nothing but text');
+  ok(/alert\(1\)/.test(evil), '(the script\'s words are shown as words)');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] connector labels apart');
+{
+  const svg = mmRender('flowchart TD\n  S[Check the request] -- approved --> A[Grant]\n  S -- rejected --> R[Deny]\n  S -- needs info --> I[Ask]\n  S -- escalated --> E[Manager]');
+  const ls = rects(svg, 'mm-edge-label');
+  let worst = 0; for (let i = 0; i < ls.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < ls.length; j++) worst = Math.max(worst, overlap(ls[i], ls[j]));
+  ok(ls.length === 4 && worst === 0, `four labels out of one node: none covers another (${ls.length} labels, worst overlap ${worst} px²)`);
+  // EACH ON ITS OWN LINE (the developer: "It's hard to tell what connector the rejected label is on"): no OTHER curve
+  // passes through or right beside a label - the curve under it is its own
+  {
+    const curves = [...svg.matchAll(/<path class="mm-edge" d="M ([-\d.]+) ([-\d.]+) C ([-\d.]+) ([-\d.]+), ([-\d.]+) ([-\d.]+), ([-\d.]+) ([-\d.]+)"/g)].map((m) => m.slice(1).map(Number));
+    const pt = (c, t) => { const u = 1 - t; return [u * u * u * c[0] + 3 * u * u * t * c[2] + 3 * u * t * t * c[4] + t * t * t * c[6], u * u * u * c[1] + 3 * u * u * t * c[3] + 3 * u * t * t * c[5] + t * t * t * c[7]]; };
+    const through = (r, c) => { for (let k = 0; k <= 64; k++) { const [x, y] = pt(c, k / 64); if (x > r.x1 - 3 && x < r.x2 + 3 && y > r.y1 - 3 && y < r.y2 + 3) return true; } return false; };
+    let crossed = 0;
+    for (const r of ls) if (curves.filter((c) => through(r, c)).length > 1) crossed++;   // (its own curve passes under it; another must not)
+    ok(curves.length === 4 && crossed === 0, `each label has only its own connector under it (${crossed} with another one through it)`);
+  }
+  const yn = rects(mmRender('flowchart TD\n  B{Paid?} -- yes --> C[Ship it]\n  B -- no --> D[Remind the buyer]'), 'mm-edge-label');
+  ok(yn.length === 2 && overlap(yn[0], yn[1]) === 0, 'yes / no out of a decision: apart');
+  const lr = rects(mmRender('flowchart LR\n  S[Start] -- a long label one --> A[One]\n  S -- a long label two --> B[Two]\n  S -- a long label three --> C[Three]'), 'mm-edge-label');
+  let w2 = 0; for (let i = 0; i < lr.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < lr.length; j++) w2 = Math.max(w2, overlap(lr[i], lr[j]));
+  ok(lr.length === 3 && w2 === 0, `left to right, three long labels: apart (worst ${w2})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] the label written into the arrow');
+{
+  const g = mmParse('flowchart LR\n  A -- yes --> B\n  B -- plain --- C\n  C -. maybe .-> D\n  D == strong ==> E\n  E -->|piped| F');
+  ok(g && g.nodes.length === 6 && g.edges.map((e) => e.style + ':' + e.label).join() === 'arrow:yes,line:plain,dotted:maybe,thick:strong,arrow:piped', `every form, its style and label (${g && g.edges.map((e) => e.style + ':' + e.label).join()})`);
+  ok(mmParse('flowchart LR\n  A -- partial', { streaming: false }).nodes.length === 1, 'a half-written one keeps what came before it');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] one line of words');
+{
+  ok(mmPlainLabel('Order<br/>placed <b>now</b> &amp; then') === 'Order placed now & then', `a label as words (${mmPlainLabel('Order<br/>placed <b>now</b> &amp; then')})`);
+  const seq = mmRender('sequenceDiagram\n  participant A as Web<br/>app\n  A->>B: send<br/>order\n  Note over A,B: <b>paid</b> first');
+  ok(!/&lt;br|&lt;b&gt;/.test(seq) && /Web app/.test(seq) && /send order/.test(seq) && /paid first/.test(seq), 'a sequence diagram\'s participants, messages and notes read as one line');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] fast');
+{
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const many = '<b>' + 'x '.repeat(50) + '</b><i>y</i><br/>'.repeat(40);
+  mmRender(`flowchart TD\n  A["${many}"] -- "<b>l</b><br/>m" --> B["<u>z</u>"]`);
+  ok(Date.now() - t0 < 500, `a label full of tags renders at once (${Date.now() - t0} ms)`);
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+const look = (page) => page.evaluate(() => ({ loading: !!document.getElementById('chatLoading'), welcome: !!document.querySelector('#log .welcome'), text: document.getElementById('log').textContent.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 120) }));
+const settle = async (page, want, ms = 8000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); let s; while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { s = await look(page); if (want(s)) return s; await sleep(150); } return s; };
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage(); await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof openProject === 'function' && document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 20000 });
+  await sleep(1500);
+
+  console.log('\n[1] a new, empty folder, again and again');
+  let stuck = 0, last = null;
+  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
+    const d = await mkProj('empty' + i);
+    await page.evaluate((p) => openProject(p), d);
+    last = await settle(page, (s) => !s.loading && s.welcome);
+    if (last.loading || !last.welcome) stuck++;
+  }
+  ok(stuck === 0, `each new empty folder ends its loading line and shows the welcome (${stuck} of 6 stuck; last ${JSON.stringify(last)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a slow restore: the first state always says "restoring"');
+  process.env.CB_TEST_RESTORE_DELAY_MS = '700';
+  let stuck2 = 0;
+  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
+    const d = await mkProj('slow' + i);
+    await page.evaluate((p) => openProject(p), d);
+    const s = await settle(page, (x) => !x.loading && x.welcome, 10000);
+    if (s.loading || !s.welcome) { stuck2++; last = s; }
+  }
+  delete process.env.CB_TEST_RESTORE_DELAY_MS;
+  ok(stuck2 === 0, `with a slow restore too (${stuck2} of 3 stuck${stuck2 ? ': ' + JSON.stringify(last) : ''})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a folder with a conversation');
+  const withChat = await mkProj('chat', true);
+  await page.evaluate((p) => openProject(p), withChat);
+  const s3 = await settle(page, (x) => !x.loading && /An old answer about chat/.test(x.text));
+  ok(!s3.loading && /An old question about chat/.test(s3.text) && /An old answer about chat/.test(s3.text), `its messages show (${JSON.stringify(s3)})`);
+} catch (e) {
+  fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e && e.stack || e);
+} finally {
+  await browser.close().catch(() => {}); server.close();
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
