=== add test/test-plugin-tell-agent-e2e.mjs
new c2489f9c180107e444d12517
+/**
+ * A PLUGIN TELLS THE AGENT, AND GETS ITS ANSWER (the developer, 2026-11-19: "I have a plugin that has an http server
+ * running and a webhook where it accepts a request, then gives to the agent and the agent gives a reply that goes back as
+ * a response. Right now in the ui, the message to the agent ... shows up as a user message, I'd like it to look like a
+ * tool"; "let's go w/ plugin call"). A plugin like theirs - an HTTP server started in init(host) - and a scripted model,
+ * in a real browser:
+ *   1. a webhook request with the agent idle: a run starts, the agent answers with replyTo, the HTTP response carries it
+ *   2. the chat shows the plugin's message as one tool row naming the plugin - never as the developer's bubble
+ *   3. a webhook request while a run is going: steered into it, answered, shown the same way
+ *   4. no answer in timeoutMs: { replied: false, timedOut: true }
+ *   5. a post to /api/message that says it is a plugin's, without the window's key, is not taken as one
+ * Run: node test/test-plugin-tell-agent-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-tell-home-'));   // plugins are global: isolate the home
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const PORT = 29797;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+// THE PLUGIN: an HTTP server on a free port; each POST { what, text, timeoutMs } is told to the agent, and the answer
+// written back as the response
+const PLUGIN = [
+  'let srv = null;',
+  'export default {',
+  '  async init(host) {',
+  '    const http = await import("node:http");',
+  '    srv = http.createServer((req, res) => {',
+  '      let b = ""; req.on("data", (c) => { b += c; });',
+  '      req.on("end", async () => {',
+  '        try {',
+  '          const j = JSON.parse(b || "{}");',
+  '          const r = await host.request("tell-agent", { text: j.text, label: "webhook: " + j.what, ...(j.timeoutMs ? { timeoutMs: j.timeoutMs } : {}) });',
+  '          res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "application/json" }); res.end(JSON.stringify(r));',
+  '        } catch (e) { res.writeHead(500); res.end(String(e && e.message || e)); }',
+  '      });',
+  '    });',
+  '    await new Promise((r) => srv.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));',
+  '    globalThis.__hookyPort = srv.address().port;',
+  '  },',
+  '  dispose() { try { srv && srv.close(); } catch {} },',
+  '};',
+].join('\n');
+
+const texts = (req) => (req && req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content)));
+let mainCalls = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const ms = texts(req);
+    const all = ms.join('\n');
+    if (!all.includes('THE CHECKLIST IS YOURS TO MARK')) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; return; }
+    mainCalls++;
+    const asked = ms.flatMap((m) => [...m.matchAll(/request (p\d+)\]/g)].filter(() => !/ignore-me/.test(m)).map((x) => x[1]));
+    const answered = new Set([...all.matchAll(/answer to request (p\d+)/g)].map((x) => x[1]));
+    const open = [...new Set(asked)].filter((id) => !answered.has(id));
+    let t;
+    if (open.length) {
+      const what = (new RegExp(`webhook: ([^\\]\\n]+?) - from the plugin hooky, request ${open[0]}\\]`).exec(all) || [])[1] || '?';
+      t = `Answering the webhook. <remote-workspace><js>return await replyTo("${open[0]}", "accepted: ${what} (real Okafor-Bright)");</js></remote-workspace>`;
+    } else if (all.includes('do the long work') && !all.includes('slept-done')) t = 'Working. <remote-workspace><js>await sleep(5000); return "slept-done";</js></remote-workspace>';
+    else t = 'Done. HOOK_FINAL_' + mainCalls + ' <no-hand-over/>';
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }

+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const hook = async (body) => { const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${globalThis.__hookyPort}/`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return r.ok ? r.json() : { error: await r.text() }; };
+  const state = async () => (await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json());
+  const idle = async (ms = 20000) => { for (let t = 0; t < ms; t += 200) { const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running') return true; await sleep(200); } return false; };
+  try {

+    ok(!!globalThis.__hookyPort, `the plugin's own server is listening (${globalThis.__hookyPort})`);

+    await idle();

+    const look = await page.evaluate(() => ({

+      bubbles: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].map((b) => b.textContent.trim().slice(0, 80)),
+    }));
+    ok(look.rows.some((t) => /⇲ webhook: order 7 — from hooky/.test(t)), `one line: "⇲ webhook: order 7 — from hooky" (${JSON.stringify(look.rows)})`);
+    ok(!look.bubbles.some((t) => /hooky|order 7/.test(t)), `no bubble of the developer's for it (${JSON.stringify(look.bubbles)})`);

+/**
+ * CODE_BOSS'S OWN COPY OF A RESULT, READ BACK (found on the box, 2026-11-18: an agent writing a plugin batched
+ * authorPlugins() sections; the result was past 48 KB and went to .code_boss/tmp/result-call_*.txt; readJson() of it
+ * masked the brief like any file - render, choice, enum, unpublish, ws.onopen, ctx.prefiltered became tokens - and the
+ * note said "60 written in the file itself, 153 in what you were given ... the file on disk holds the real values ...
+ * this is code_boss's own copy of an earlier result, kept masked as you were shown it").
+ * Through the real worker with the shipped filter (piishape), CB_PII_AT_RETURN on (the default):
+ *   [1] three sections of the brief returned together go to the file; readJson() of it hands over the brief exactly as
+ *       it is on disk - the API names whole, no token added - and the note says that, with no "real values" claim
+ *   [2] read() and search() of it, the same
+ *   [3] the control: the same words in a project file ARE masked (so [1] shows the copy is exempt, not the filter idle)
+ *   [4] the copy changed after code_boss wrote it (a snippet's write): masked as any file, the personal value in it a
+ *       token, and the note says it is not a copy code_boss can vouch for
+ *   [5] a snippet's log in .code_boss/logs/, read back: handed over as it is, as many tokens as on disk
+ * Run: node test/test-result-copy-readback.mjs
+ */

+import path from 'node:path';
+

+const { authoringBrief } = await import('../src/plugins/authoring.mjs');
+const { ANY_TOKEN_RE } = await import('../src/tokens.mjs');
+

+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };

+const block = (js) => `<remote-workspace><js>${js}</js></remote-workspace>`;

+const SSN = '482-11-9037';
+

+const filter = filterOf();
+
+async function run(snippets) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const replies = [...snippets.map((s) => (typeof s === 'function' ? (shown) => block(s(shown)) : block(s))), 'Done.\n' + C];
+  const sent = [];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { const shown = JSON.stringify(req.messages); sent.push(shown); const r = replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)]; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: typeof r === 'function' ? r(shown) : r } }] }; } };
+  const events = [];
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: dir,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {}, contentFilter: filter });
+  return { results: events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval'), sent };

+const val = (r) => { const s = String(r.content || ''); const i = s.indexOf('\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n'); return i < 0 ? s : s.slice(0, i); };
+const record = (r) => { const s = String(r.content || ''); const i = s.indexOf('\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n'); return i < 0 ? '' : s.slice(i); };

+/**
+ * THE CONTAINER MANAGER ON code_boss's ONE PROCESS (ONE-PROCESS-SPEC.md 1, "Who starts it" - the container): App.java
+ * starts the host once and asks it for a server per instance. No container here: the manager's App is driven by a small
+ * Java driver in its own package, with the container's part (App.Io - start a process inside, publish a port, clear a
+ * port) swapped for a stand-in, and a stand-in HOST - an HTTP server answering /host/* as the spec says - recording
+ * every call the manager makes.
+ *   [1] the first instance starts the host once (CB_HOST_PORT, CB_HOST_KEY, CB_IN_CONTAINER), waits for /host/health,
+ *       then POST /host/servers with what was its environment, the key on every request
+ *   [2] a second instance: only a POST - the host is not started again
+ *   [3] /host/health is each instance's windows and project
+ *   [4] stopping one: DELETE /host/servers/<id>, the host left running
+ *   [5] restarting one from its window: DELETE then POST of that server alone, with why
+ *   [6] a server the host no longer lists closed itself: that instance is stopped, nothing else
+ *   [7] the host silent for the auto-restart time: the HOST restarted (asked to quit, ended), a new key, every instance
+ *       asked of it again with why
+ *   [8] the host crashing: started again, every instance asked of it again
+ *   [9] a window's restart while the host is not answering: the host restarted
+ *  [10] the container stopping: POST /host/quit, every instance stopped
+ * Needs a JDK. Run: node test/test-sandbox-host-manager.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { sblFindJdk, sblWrite, sblCompileArgs, sblJavacMajor } from '../src/agent/sandbox-launcher.mjs';
+

+if (jc.status !== 0) { console.log('FAIL javac: ' + String(jc.stderr || jc.stdout).slice(0, 1200)); process.exit(1); }

+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.Collections;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+public final class HostDriver {
+  static int pass, fail;
+  static void ok(boolean c, String l) { if (c) { pass++; System.out.println("  ok " + l); } else { fail++; System.out.println("  FAIL " + l); } System.out.flush(); }
+  static void head(String t) { System.out.println(); System.out.println(t); System.out.flush(); }
+
+  // ── the stand-in host ──
+  static final List<String> calls = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<String>());   // every request but /host/health, in order
+  static final List<Map<String, Object>> posts = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<Map<String, Object>>());
+  static final Map<String, Map<String, Object>> servers = Collections.synchronizedMap(new LinkedHashMap<String, Map<String, Object>>());
+  static final List<Map<String, String>> envs = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<Map<String, String>>());
+  static final List<String> published = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<String>());
+  static volatile String key = "";
+  static volatile boolean silent;
+  static volatile int seq, refused, healths;
+  static volatile Fake proc;
+  static int fakePort;
+
+  static final class Fake implements App.HostProc {
+    volatile Integer exit;
+    volatile boolean terminated;
+    public Integer waitExit(int ms) { long end = System.currentTimeMillis() + ms; while (exit == null && System.currentTimeMillis() < end) Sandbox.sleep(20); return exit; }
+    public void terminateTree() { terminated = true; if (exit == null) exit = Integer.valueOf(1); }
+    public void close() { }
+  }
+
+  static final class FakeIo implements App.Io {
+    public String drive() { return "Z:"; }
+    public App.HostProc startHost(Map<String, String> env, String z) {
+      envs.add(new LinkedHashMap<String, String>(env));
+      key = env.get("CB_HOST_KEY");
+      servers.clear();
+      silent = false;
+      Fake p = new Fake();
+      proc = p;
+      return p;
+    }
+    public int publishHost(int inside) { published.add("host " + inside); return fakePort; }
+    public void publish(int o, int i) { published.add(o + "->" + i); }
+    public void unpublish(int o) { published.add("-" + o); }
+    public Container.Cleared clearPort(int port, int spare) { Container.Cleared c = new Container.Cleared(); c.free = true; return c; }
+  }
+
+  static void send(HttpExchange x, int code, Object v) throws java.io.IOException {
+    byte[] b = Json.write(v).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+    x.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", "application/json");
+    x.sendResponseHeaders(code, b.length);
+    x.getResponseBody().write(b);
+    x.close();
+  }

+  static final class Host implements HttpHandler {
+    public void handle(HttpExchange x) throws java.io.IOException {
+      String m = x.getRequestMethod(), p = x.getRequestURI().getPath(), q = x.getRequestURI().getRawQuery();
+      java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream bo = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
+      byte[] buf = new byte[4096];
+      for (int n; (n = x.getRequestBody().read(buf)) > 0; ) bo.write(buf, 0, n);
+      String body = new String(bo.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
+      Fake f = proc;
+      if (f == null || f.exit != null) { x.close(); return; }                 // a host that has ended answers nothing
+      if (silent) { Sandbox.sleep(5000); x.close(); return; }                  // nor does one that is held
+      if (!key.equals(x.getRequestHeaders().getFirst("X-CB-Host-Key"))) { refused++; send(x, 403, Collections.singletonMap("error", "no key")); return; }
+      if ("GET".equals(m) && "/host/health".equals(p)) {
+        healths++;
+        Map<String, Object> h = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        h.put("pid", Integer.valueOf(4242)); h.put("build", "9999"); h.put("uptime", Integer.valueOf(1));
+        synchronized (servers) { h.put("servers", new ArrayList<Object>(servers.values())); }
+        send(x, 200, h);
+        return;
+      }
+      calls.add(m + " " + p + (q == null ? "" : "?" + q));
+      if ("POST".equals(m) && "/host/servers".equals(p)) {
+        Map<String, Object> b = Json.object(Json.parse(body));
+        posts.add(b);
+        String id = "s" + (++seq);
+        Map<String, Object> s = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        s.put("id", id); s.put("port", b.get("port")); s.put("project", ""); s.put("windows", Integer.valueOf(0)); s.put("status", "listening"); s.put("busy", Boolean.FALSE);
+        servers.put(id, s);
+        Map<String, Object> r = new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+        r.put("id", id); r.put("port", b.get("port"));
+        send(x, 200, r);
+        return;
+      }
+      if ("DELETE".equals(m) && p.startsWith("/host/servers/")) {
+        boolean had = servers.remove(p.substring("/host/servers/".length())) != null;
+        send(x, had ? 200 : 404, Collections.singletonMap("ok", Boolean.valueOf(had)));
+        return;
+      }
+      if ("POST".equals(m) && "/host/quit".equals(p)) {
+        send(x, 200, Collections.singletonMap("ok", Boolean.TRUE));
+        f.exit = Integer.valueOf(0);
+        return;
+      }
+      send(x, 404, Collections.singletonMap("error", "no such call"));
+    }
+  }

+  interface Cond { boolean ok(); }
+  static boolean until(Cond c, long ms) { long end = System.currentTimeMillis() + ms; while (System.currentTimeMillis() < end) { if (c.ok()) return true; Sandbox.sleep(100); } return c.ok(); }
+  static List<String> callsFrom(int from) { synchronized (calls) { return new ArrayList<String>(calls.subList(Math.min(from, calls.size()), calls.size())); } }
+  static int indexOf(List<String> l, String prefix) { for (int i = 0; i < l.size(); i++) if (l.get(i).startsWith(prefix)) return i; return -1; }
+  static App.Inst inst(int id) { return App.inst(id) != null && App.inst(id).id == id ? App.inst(id) : null; }
+  static boolean running(int id) { App.Inst i = inst(id); return i != null && "running".equals(i.state) && !i.restarting && !i.hostRestarting; }

+/**
+ * THE AGENT MARKS THE CHECKLIST (the developer, 2026-11-19: "make it where the main agent marks the checklist items as
+ * done instead of the bookkeeper. It's a small task and will make the checklist real time and then we can make the
+ * active item a loading animation while the agent is running"). A scripted model whose snippet calls todo(2, "doing"),
+ * then todo(2, "done") and todo(3, "doing"), each followed by a pause - in a real browser:
+ *   1. the checklist at the end of the chat follows each mark while the snippet is still running
+ *   2. the item under way turns (tl-spin) while the run goes on, and stops turning when the run ends
+ *   3. the marks are in stream-marks.json, made by the agent (byAgent)
+ *   4. the agent's prompt teaches todo() and lists it among the functions
+ * Run: node test/test-todo-agent-marks-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';

+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const PORT = 29795;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const MARKS = {
+  v: 2, seq: 1, nextTodo: 4, marked: 'e2', changes: [], refused: [], faded: {}, bkFaded: {}, items: { e1: { kind: 'request' } },
+  todos: [
+    { id: 1, from: 'e1', after: null, state: 'done', text: 'read the formatter' },
+    { id: 2, from: 'e1', after: null, state: 'planned', text: 'write the fix' },

+const prompts = [];

+  // the main agent's calls only: the bookkeeper's and the other agents' get "ok" (no ops)

+    if (!all.includes('THE CHECKLIST IS YOURS TO MARK')) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; return; }
+    prompts.push(all);
+    const t = TURNS[Math.min(call++, TURNS.length - 1)];
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};

+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }

=== add test/test-zip-read.mjs
new f4ff5caebfb15c40d9bcecd3
+/**
+ * WHAT IS IN A JAR OR A ZIP, READ IN PLACE (src/agent/zip-read.mjs; found on the box, 2026-11-18: an agent wanted the
+ * classes in a dependency jar and could only run jar.exe through a node child script, which failed):
+ *   [1] the entries of an archive built here - stored and deflated, a folder, a UTF-8 name - with their sizes
+ *   [2] one entry's bytes, either way packed; a name not there says so, and what it may have meant
+ *   [3] not an archive, and a broken one: said plainly, not a crash
+ *   [4] a real jar (from the Maven repository, when there is one): the same entries `jar tf` lists
+ * Run: node test/test-zip-read.mjs
+ */
+import { deflateRawSync, crc32 } from 'node:zlib';
+import { readdirSync, readFileSync, existsSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { homedir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { zipEntries, zipEntry } from '../src/agent/zip-read.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const throws = (f) => { try { f(); return ''; } catch (e) { return String(e.message || e); } };
+const rejects = async (f) => { try { await f(); return ''; } catch (e) { return String(e.message || e); } };
+
+// a zip, written here: [{ name, data, deflate }]
+function makeZip(files) {
+  const locals = [], cens = []; let off = 0;
+  for (const f of files) {
+    const name = Buffer.from(f.name, 'utf8'), data = Buffer.from(f.data || ''), packed = f.deflate ? deflateRawSync(data) : data;
+    const flags = /[^\x00-\x7f]/.test(f.name) ? 0x800 : 0, method = f.deflate ? 8 : 0, crc = crc32(data);
+    const lh = Buffer.alloc(30); lh.writeUInt32LE(0x04034b50, 0); lh.writeUInt16LE(20, 4); lh.writeUInt16LE(flags, 6); lh.writeUInt16LE(method, 8); lh.writeUInt32LE(crc, 14); lh.writeUInt32LE(packed.length, 18); lh.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 22); lh.writeUInt16LE(name.length, 26);
+    const ch = Buffer.alloc(46); ch.writeUInt32LE(0x02014b50, 0); ch.writeUInt16LE(20, 4); ch.writeUInt16LE(20, 6); ch.writeUInt16LE(flags, 8); ch.writeUInt16LE(method, 10); ch.writeUInt32LE(crc, 16); ch.writeUInt32LE(packed.length, 20); ch.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 24); ch.writeUInt16LE(name.length, 28); ch.writeUInt32LE(off, 42);
+    locals.push(lh, name, packed); cens.push(ch, name); off += 30 + name.length + packed.length;
+  }
+  const cen = Buffer.concat(cens), end = Buffer.alloc(22);
+  end.writeUInt32LE(0x06054b50, 0); end.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 8); end.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 10); end.writeUInt32LE(cen.length, 12); end.writeUInt32LE(off, 16);
+  return Buffer.concat([...locals, cen, end]);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[1] the entries');
+const props = 'router.timeout=30\nrouter.validate=true\n';
+const big = 'class body '.repeat(500);
+const zip = makeZip([
+  { name: 'META-INF/', data: '' },
+  { name: 'META-INF/MANIFEST.MF', data: 'Manifest-Version: 1.0\r\nMain-Class: mil.cuf.Router\r\n', deflate: true },
+  { name: 'mil/cuf/CufRouter.class', data: big, deflate: true },
+  { name: 'config/router.properties', data: props },
+  { name: 'docs/Überblick.txt', data: 'hallo' },
+]);
+const es = zipEntries(zip);
+ok(es.map((e) => e.name).join() === 'META-INF/,META-INF/MANIFEST.MF,mil/cuf/CufRouter.class,config/router.properties,docs/Überblick.txt', `every entry, in order (${es.map((e) => e.name).join(', ')})`);
+ok(es[0].dir && !es[1].dir && es[2].size === big.length && es[2].csize < big.length, 'a folder is a folder; a deflated entry has its real size and its packed one');
+ok(es[4].name === 'docs/Überblick.txt', 'a UTF-8 name reads as it was written');
+
+console.log('\n[2] one entry');
+ok((await zipEntry(zip, 'config/router.properties')).toString() === props, 'a stored entry: its bytes');
+ok((await zipEntry(zip, 'mil/cuf/CufRouter.class')).toString() === big, 'a deflated one: inflated');
+ok(/Main-Class: mil\.cuf\.Router/.test((await zipEntry(zip, 'meta-inf/manifest.mf')).toString()), 'found without minding case, or a leading slash');
+ok(/"mil\/cuf\/Router\.class" is not in the archive - did you mean: mil\/cuf\/CufRouter\.class/.test(await rejects(() => zipEntry(zip, 'mil/cuf/Router.class'))), 'a name not there says so, and what it may have meant');
+ok(/is a folder/.test(await rejects(() => zipEntry(zip, 'META-INF/'))), 'a folder is not read as a file');
+
+console.log('\n[3] not an archive, or a broken one');
+ok(/not a zip or jar/.test(throws(() => zipEntries(Buffer.from('just some text, not a zip at all - long enough to look')))), 'text: "not a zip or jar"');
+const broken = Buffer.from(zip); broken.writeUInt32LE(0, zip.indexOf(Buffer.from([0x50, 0x4b, 0x01, 0x02])));   // (the first directory record's signature gone)
+ok(/broken zip|not a zip/.test(throws(() => zipEntries(broken))), 'a broken directory: said, not a crash');
+
+console.log('\n[4] a real jar');
+{
+  const repo = path.join(homedir(), '.m2', 'repository');
+  let jar = null;
+  const walk = (d, depth) => { if (jar || depth > 6) return; let xs = []; try { xs = readdirSync(d); } catch { return; } for (const x of xs) { const f = path.join(d, x); if (x.endsWith('.jar')) { jar = f; return; } try { if (statSync(f).isDirectory()) walk(f, depth + 1); } catch {} } };
+  if (existsSync(repo)) walk(repo, 0);
+  const tf = jar ? spawnSync('jar', ['tf', jar], { encoding: 'utf8' }) : null;
+  if (!jar || !tf || tf.status !== 0) console.log('  (no jar or no jar tool here - skipped)');
+  else {
+    const mine = zipEntries(readFileSync(jar)).map((e) => e.name), theirs = tf.stdout.split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean);
+    ok(mine.length === theirs.length && mine.every((n, i) => n === theirs[i]), `${path.basename(jar)}: the same ${theirs.length} entries jar tf lists`);
+  }
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] in a snippet: zipList, zipRead - a jar outside the project');
+{
+  const { mkdtemp, writeFile } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+  const { tmpdir } = await import('node:os');
+  process.env.CB_HOME = process.env.CB_HOME || await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-zipread-home-'));
+  const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-zipread-proj-')), outside = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-zipread-m2-'));
+  const jarPath = path.join(outside, 'cuf-router-7.0.1.6.jar');
+  await writeFile(jarPath, zip);
+  const snip = async (code) => { const r = String(extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, { projectDir: proj, cwd: proj }))); try { return JSON.parse(r); } catch { return r; } };
+  const J = JSON.stringify(jarPath);
+  const all = await snip(`return await zipList(${J});`);
+  ok(Array.isArray(all) && all.length === 4 && all.includes('mil/cuf/CufRouter.class') && !all.includes('META-INF/'), `zipList: the files, no folders (${JSON.stringify(all)})`);
+  const cls = await snip(`return await zipList(${J}, "*.class");`);
+  ok(Array.isArray(cls) && cls.length === 1 && cls[0] === 'mil/cuf/CufRouter.class', `zipList(path, "*.class") (${JSON.stringify(cls)})`);
+  const sub = await snip(`return await zipList(${J}, "router");`);
+  ok(Array.isArray(sub) && sub.length === 2, `zipList(path, "router"): a part of the name (${JSON.stringify(sub)})`);
+  const props = await snip(`return await zipRead(${J}, "config/router.properties");`);
+  ok(/router\.timeout=30/.test(String(props)) && /router\.validate=true/.test(String(props)), `zipRead: an entry's text (${JSON.stringify(String(props).slice(0, 60))})`);
+  const asClass = String(await snip(`return await zipRead(${J}, "mil/cuf/CufRouter.class");`));
+  ok(/a class is compiled code/.test(asClass) && /javap\(\["-cp", .*"-p", "mil\.cuf\.CufRouter"\]\)/.test(asClass), `a class: it says how javap shows it (${asClass.slice(0, 160)})`);
+  const missing = String(await snip(`return await zipRead(${J}, "mil/cuf/Router.class.txt");`));
+  ok(/is not in the archive/.test(missing), `an entry not there: said (${missing.slice(0, 120)})`);
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
